<span id="S001-04"></span>
**Source:** p.1 S001-04

**Original:**

Abstract
Flow matching models have emerged as a dominant paradigm for generative poste-
rior sampling in imaging inverse problems. Existing solvers follow two distinct
lines of work: trajectory-space methods, which refine sampling dynamics from
random initial noise, and source-space methods, which optimize the initial noise
itself. While most prior work treats these as independent design choices, we show
that they are inherently coupled. We derive a unified stability bound that links
initialization proximity and cumulative sampling stochasticity through a multiplica-
tive envelope, complemented by two empirical observations. First, we identify a
sufficiency regime in which a moderately optimized proximal source initialization
is enough to attain the performance plateau, rendering exhaustive source-space
optimization unnecessary. Second, this initial advantage is fragile: it is eroded
by excessive stochasticity accumulation during guided sampling, necessitating a
mechanism that bounds the cumulative noise injection. These findings motivate
FLINT, a two-stage framework that operationalizes this coupling. FLINT estab-
lishes a high-fidelity starting point via a manifold-constrained proximal objective in
only a few iterations, then performs guided sampling under a bounded stochasticity
schedule that helps prevent erasure of the initialization advantage. Across diverse
inverse problems, FLINT delivers state of the art perceptual quality and competitive
distortion metrics while maintaining superior computational efficiency.

**中文:**

## 摘要

Flow Matching（流匹配）模型已成为成像反问题中生成式后验采样的主导范式。现有求解器沿两条不同的研究路线展开：**轨迹空间（trajectory-space）方法**从随机初始噪声出发精化采样动力学，**源空间（source-space）方法**则优化初始噪声本身。多数先前工作把二者视为彼此独立的设计选择，而我们证明它们本质上是耦合的。我们推导出一个统一的稳定性界，借助乘性包络（multiplicative envelope）把初始化临近度（initialization proximity）与累积采样随机性联系起来，并辅以两点经验观察。第一，我们识别出一个**充分性区间（sufficiency regime）**：适度优化过的近端源初始化已足以达到性能平台（performance plateau），从而使穷尽式的源空间优化变得不必要。第二，这一初始优势是脆弱的：它会被引导采样过程中过度的随机性累积所侵蚀，因此需要一种约束累积噪声注入的机制。这些发现促成了 FLINT，一个把上述耦合操作化的两阶段框架。FLINT 仅通过少数几次迭代，用流形约束的近端目标函数建立高保真起点，随后在有界随机性调度下执行引导采样，以帮助防止初始化优势被抹除。在多种反问题上，FLINT 在保持出色计算效率的同时，取得了最先进的感知质量与有竞争力的失真指标。

1 Introduction

**中文:**

## 1 引言

---

<span id="S001-06"></span>
**Source:** p.1 S001-06

**Original:**

Inverse problems arise throughout computational imaging and computer vision [5, 22, 37, 34, 67],
where the goal is to recover a clean imagex0 ∈R d from a degraded measurement y=Ax 0 +n, with
A denoting the forward operator and n∼ N(0, σ 2
yI) additive Gaussian noise. Since A is typically
many-to-one, the problem is ill-posed and requires a prior over natural images [55, 48, 57, 56]. From
a Bayesian perspective, this reduces to sampling from the posterior p(x0 |y)∝p(y|x 0)·p(x 0),
where the likelihoodp(y|x 0)encodes the forward model and the priorp(x 0)regularizes solutions
toward the natural image manifold. Deep generative models have emerged as powerful priors with
diffusion models [21, 54] and flow matching models [33, 32]. Large-scale rectified flow models [18]
achieve photorealistic generation quality, rendering them highly attractive for posterior sampling.
Existing inverse problem solvers leveraging these priors typically fall into two categories (Sec. 2.2).

**中文:**

反问题广泛存在于计算成像与计算机视觉中 [5, 22, 37, 34, 67]，其目标是从退化观测 $y = Ax_0 + n$ 中恢复干净图像 $x_0 \in \mathbb{R}^d$，其中 $A$ 表示前向算子，$n \sim \mathcal{N}(0, \sigma_y^2 I)$ 为加性高斯噪声。由于 $A$ 通常是多对一的，该问题是不适定的，需要关于自然图像的先验 [55, 48, 57, 56]。从贝叶斯视角看，这归结为从后验 $p(x_0 \mid y) \propto p(y \mid x_0)\cdot p(x_0)$ 中采样，其中似然 $p(y \mid x_0)$ 编码前向模型，先验 $p(x_0)$ 把解正则化到自然图像流形附近。深度生成模型已成为强有力的先验，包括扩散模型 [21, 54] 与 Flow Matching 模型 [33, 32]。大规模整流流（rectified flow）模型 [18] 达到照片级的生成质量，使其对后验采样极具吸引力。

---

<span id="S001-07"></span>
**Source:** p.1 S001-07

**Original:**

Trajectory-space methods[ 11, 52, 25, 51, 66, 27, 17, 42, 29, 40, 3] steer the sampling path toward the
measurement-consistent manifold at each step but rely on a fixed, random starting pointx1 ∼ N(0,I) ,
leaving reconstruction quality inherently sensitive to the specific realization of the initial noise
(Fig. 1(a) and (b)).Source-space methods[ 8, 60, 4, 44, 23, 62, 65, 39] perform inference directly
on the initial noise x1, keeping the subsequent generative trajectory unmodified (Fig. 1(c) and (d)).
Most prior work has treated initialization and sampling dynamics as decoupled modules, an implicit

<span id="S002-C1"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S002-C1

**Original:**

Figure 1:Illustration of generative paradigms for inverse problems. (a) and (b) depict trajectory-
space methods, while panels (c) and (d) depict source-space optimization methods. (a) Likelihood
score corrections are applied directly at each noisy intermediate state xt. (b) Likelihood score
corrections are applied in clean image space via the Tweedie estimate ˆx0|t, followed by renoising
to the generative manifold at the next timestep. (c) Source-space MAP methods reparameterize
the clean image via the deterministic generative map Φ as x0 = Φ(x 1) and optimize over the
initial noise x1 to minimize data fidelity. (d) Source-space posterior sampling draws samples from
p(x1 |y) via Hamiltonian Monte Carlo or Langevin dynamics. (e) By strategically coupling proximal
source initialization with bounded stochasticity during guided posterior sampling, FLINT bounds the
sampling trajectory, preserving the initialization advantage and yielding high-fidelity convergence.

**中文:**

**图 1：反问题的生成式范式示意。** (a) 与 (b) 为轨迹空间方法，(c) 与 (d) 为源空间优化方法。(a) 似然得分修正直接施加在每个含噪中间状态 $x_t$ 上。(b) 似然得分修正在干净图像空间中经由 Tweedie 估计 $\hat{x}_{0|t}$ 施加，随后重新加噪回到生成流形上的下一个时间步。(c) 源空间 MAP 方法通过确定性生成映射 $\Phi$ 把干净图像重参数化为 $x_0 = \Phi(x_1)$，并对初始噪声 $x_1$ 优化以最小化数据保真度。(d) 源空间后验采样通过哈密顿蒙特卡洛或 Langevin 动力学从 $p(x_1 \mid y)$ 中抽取样本。(e) 在引导后验采样过程中策略性地耦合近端源初始化与有界随机性，FLINT 因而约束了采样轨迹，保持初始化优势并获得高保真收敛。

<span id="S002-04"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S002-04

**Original:**

In this work, we reveal that these two design choices are not independent but inherently coupled,
and that exploiting their synergy is beneficial for high-fidelity posterior sampling. Combining a
stability bound that decomposes into a source-space initialization term and a cumulative stochasticity
term with empirical observations, we identify two complementary findings. First, a proximal source
initialization that anchors the starting noise within a sufficient proximity of a source-space solution
can attain high-quality reconstruction without exhaustive optimization. Second, the benefits of
this proximal start are fragile. They are eroded by the excessive accumulation of stochasticity
during guided posterior sampling, necessitating a mechanism to bound the cumulative injected
noise. Together, these findings lead toFLINT( Flow-based posterior sampling with proximal source
initialization and bounded stochasticity), a two-stage framework that operationalizes this coupling.
Stage 1 implements a well-informed starting point by optimizing a manifold-constrained proximal
objective for only a few iterations. Stage 2 then employs a bounded stochasticity schedule to steer
the trajectory while preventing the erasure of the informed initialization (Fig. 1(e)). Our main
contributions are:

**中文:**

在本工作中，我们揭示这两个设计选择并非相互独立，而是本质耦合的，并且利用其协同效应对高保真后验采样是有益的。把可分解为源空间初始化项与累积随机性项的稳定性界同经验观察结合起来，我们得到两点互补的发现。第一，一个把起始噪声锚定在源空间解的足够近邻域内的近端源初始化，无需穷尽优化即可获得高质量重建。第二，这种近端起点的收益是脆弱的。它会被引导后验采样过程中过度的随机性累积所侵蚀，因此需要一种约束累积注入噪声的机制。这些发现共同导出 FLINT（Flow-based posterior sampling with proximal source initialization and bounded stochasticity，即“带近端源初始化与有界随机性的基于流的后验采样”），一个把该耦合操作化的两阶段框架。第 1 阶段仅通过少数几次迭代优化流形约束的近端目标函数，从而构造信息充分的起点。第 2 阶段随后采用有界随机性调度来引导轨迹，同时防止这种有信息量的初始化被抹除（图 1(e)）。我们的主要贡献如下：

> 原文 “lead toFLINT” 因粗体排版丢失空格，译文中按 “lead to FLINT” 理解。

<span id="S002-05"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S002-05

**Original:**

• Theoretical Coupling:We derive a unified stability bound for flow-based posterior sampling,
showing that the terminal deviation from a deterministic reference is controlled by initialization
proximity and cumulative sampling stochasticity. We empirically identify a sufficiency regime,
where moderate source-space optimization is enough to reach the performance plateau.

**中文:**

- **理论耦合（Theoretical Coupling）：** 我们为基于流的后验采样推导出一个统一稳定性界，表明相对确定性参考的终端偏差由初始化临近度与累积采样随机性共同控制。我们从经验上识别出一个充分性区间：适度的源空间优化即足以达到性能平台。

> 贡献列表第 1 条。

---

<span id="S002-06"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S002-06

**Original:**

• FLINT Framework:We introduce FLINT, a training-free two-stage sampler that bridges source-
space and trajectory-space methods. It utilizes a manifold-constrained proximal objective for
informed initialization and a bounded stochasticity schedule that helps preserve the initialization
advantage during guided posterior sampling.

**中文:**

- **FLINT 框架：** 我们提出 FLINT，一个无需训练的两阶段采样器，桥接源空间方法与轨迹空间方法。它利用流形约束的近端目标函数获得有信息量的初始化，并采用有界随机性调度，帮助在引导后验采样过程中保持初始化优势。

> 贡献列表第 2 条。

---

<span id="S002-07"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S002-07

**Original:**

• Experimental Results:Extensive experiments across imaging inverse problems show that FLINT
achieves state-of-the-art perceptual quality and competitive or superior distortion metrics, with a
markedly favorable efficiency-performance profile.

**中文:**

- **实验结果：** 在成像反问题上的大量实验表明，FLINT 取得了最先进的感知质量，以及有竞争力或更优的失真指标，并具有明显更优的效率–性能折衷。

> 贡献列表第 3 条。

**Original:**

Samples are generated by integrating the ODE from t= 1 to t= 0 over a fixed schedule {τ (N)
i }N

with N∈[28,50] steps in practice. We denote the resulting deterministic generative map as

single step clean image estimate from any intermediate state [26].

**中文:**

样本通过从 $t = 1$ 到 $t = 0$ 在固定时间表 $\{\tau_i^{(N)}\}_{i=1}^{N}$ 上积分该 ODE 生成，实践中步数 $N \in [28, 50]$。我们把所得的确定性生成映射记为 $\Phi: \mathbb{R}^d \to \mathbb{R}^d$，于是 $x_0 = \Phi(x_1)$。对任意中间状态 $x_t$ 处的线性插值作整理，可得到基于流的 Tweedie 公式 $\hat{x}_{0|t} := \mathbb{E}[x_0 \mid x_t] \approx x_t - t\,v_\theta(x_t, t)$，它可由任意中间状态给出单步干净图像估计 [26]。

---

<span id="S003-04"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-04

**Original:**

2.2 Posterior Sampling for Inverse Problems

**中文:**

### 2.2 反问题的后验采样

---

<span id="S003-05"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-05

**Original:**

Given the measurement model y=Ax 0 +n , posterior sampling aims to draw samples from
p(x0 |y)∝p(y|x 0)p(x 0) [54, 53, 11, 52, 12]. Existing solvers built on diffusion or flow
matching priors divide into two families.Trajectory-space methodssteer intermediate states toward
measurement consistency via the Tweedie estimate ˆx0|t :=E[x 0 |x t], obtaining the posterior
mean either via Tweedie’s formula or by integrating a probability flow ODE fromxt toward t= 0 .
Within this family, one subfamily enforces data consistency directly in the noisy latent space by
approximating ∇xt logp(y|x t)≈ ∇ xt logp(y| ˆx0|t) [11, 52, 25, 10, 47, 46, 41, 30], while the
other enforces consistency in clean image space via ˆx0|t and then renoises back to the (t+∆t) -
manifold of the generative prior [ 51, 66, 28, 27, 17, 42, 40, 29, 3]. Both subfamilies initialize
from x1 ∼ N(0,I) , leaving the quality of the starting point entirely uncontrolled.

**中文:**

给定观测模型 $y = Ax_0 + n$，后验采样旨在从 $p(x_0 \mid y) \propto p(y \mid x_0)p(x_0)$ 中抽取样本 [54, 53, 11, 52, 12]。建立在扩散或 Flow Matching 先验上的现有求解器分为两族。**轨迹空间方法** 通过 Tweedie 估计 $\hat{x}_{0|t} := \mathbb{E}[x_0 \mid x_t]$ 把中间状态引向与观测一致，其方式或直接使用 Tweedie 公式，或从 $x_t$ 向 $t = 0$ 积分概率流 ODE 得到后验均值。在该族中，一个子类在含噪隐空间直接施加数据一致性，做法是近似 $\nabla_{x_t} \log p(y \mid x_t) \approx \nabla_{x_t} \log p(y \mid \hat{x}_{0|t})$ [11, 52, 25, 10, 47, 46, 41, 30]；另一子类则在干净图像空间中经由 $\hat{x}_{0|t}$ 施加一致性，再重新加噪回到生成先验的 $(t+\Delta t)$ 流形 [51, 66, 28, 27, 17, 42, 40, 29, 3]。两个子类都从 $x_1 \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I)$ 初始化，使起点的质量完全不受控制。

> 段落按语义在 “Source-space methods” 处与 S003-06 切分。

---

<span id="S003-06"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-06

**Original:**

Source-space
methods instead confine to the initial noise x1 via the deterministic generative map Φ :R d →R d
defined by the full reverse ODE, leaving the generative trajectory unmodified. One line of work casts
source-space inference as MAP estimation [8, 60, 4], reparameterizing x0 = Φ(x1) and optimizing
x1 to minimize data fidelity. Another targets p(x1 |y) directly via Langevin dynamics, Hamiltonian
Monte Carlo, or related MCMC updates [44, 23, 62, 65, 39], enabling broader posterior exploration.
Both subfamilies nonetheless share a substantial computational burden: evaluating ∇x1 J(Φ(x 1))
requires backpropagating through the fullN-step reverse ODE.

**中文:**

**源空间方法** 则把推断限定在初始噪声 $x_1$ 上，通过由完整反向 ODE 定义的确定性生成映射 $\Phi: \mathbb{R}^d \to \mathbb{R}^d$ 实现，并保持生成轨迹不变。一类工作把源空间推断表述为 MAP 估计 [8, 60, 4]，将 $x_0 = \Phi(x_1)$ 重参数化并对 $x_1$ 优化以最小化数据保真度。另一类则通过 Langevin 动力学、哈密顿蒙特卡洛或相关 MCMC 更新直接面向 $p(x_1 \mid y)$ [44, 23, 62, 65, 39]，从而实现更广泛的后验探索。然而两个子类都承担可观的计算负担：计算 $\nabla_{x_1} J(\Phi(x_1))$ 需要沿完整的 $N$ 步反向 ODE 反向传播。

> 原文 “methods instead” 因粗体排版缺空格，已在 original_text 中补一个空格。

---

<span id="S003-07"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-07

**Original:**

2.3 Reducing the Computational Cost of Source-Space Optimization

**中文:**

### 2.3 降低源空间优化的计算开销

<span id="S003-08"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-08

**Original:**

Source-space methods optimize x1 by minimizing a data consistency objective J(x 0). Computing
∇x1 J(x 0) incurs two compounding costs: obtaining x0 requires an N-step reverse ODE, and
backpropagating through this chain requires evaluating the Jacobian productQ
i
∂xi−1
∂xi
.

**中文:**

源空间方法通过最小化数据一致性目标 $J(x_0)$ 来优化 $x_1$。计算 $\nabla_{x_1} J(x_0)$ 会带来两项叠加的开销：得到 $x_0$ 需要 $N$ 步反向 ODE，而沿这条链反向传播又需要计算雅可比乘积 $\prod_i \frac{\partial x_{i-1}}{\partial x_i}$。

steps [49, 31]. The flow ODE is integrated for the first K−1 steps along the N-step time schedule
{τ (N)
i }N
i=1, after which a clean image estimate is formed via the flow-based Tweedie’s formula:

<span id="S003-12"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-12

**Original:**

Efficient gradient computation.The backpropagation cost has been mitigated by strategies such as
adjoint methods and gradient checkpointing [38, 58, 7, 43, 14], though these often trade memory for
computation. Shortcut Diffusion Optimization (SDO) [16] reinterprets sampling as Picard fixed-point
iteration and shows that ∇x1 J(x 0) is well-approximated by ∂J
∂x0
∂xN−1
∂x1
, retaining the computational
graph only at the first denoising step with bounded error. Our method combines truncated sampling
and the SDO shortcut, as detailed in Algorithm 2, to substantially reduce per-iteration overhead.

**中文:**

**高效梯度计算（Efficient gradient computation）。** 反向传播开销已通过伴随方法与梯度检查点等策略得到缓解 [38, 58, 7, 43, 14]，但这些方法常常以内存换计算。Shortcut Diffusion Optimization（SDO）[16] 把采样重新解释为 Picard 不动点迭代，并表明 $\nabla_{x_1} J(x_0)$ 可由 $\frac{\partial J}{\partial x_0}\,\frac{\partial x_{N-1}}{\partial x_1}$ 很好地近似，仅在第一个去噪步保留计算图且误差有界。我们的方法结合截断采样与 SDO 捷径，详见算法 2，从而大幅降低每次迭代的开销。

Figure 2:Interplay of proximal initialization and sampling stochasticityon Gaussian deblurring.
(a) PSNR and LPIPS as functions of the perturbation scale C0 across varying the cumulative stochas-
ticity G, demonstrating the existence of a sufficiency regime (C0 ≤0.4 in this setup) where proximal
initialization performs on par with exact inversion. (b) Visual comparisons for C0 ∈ {0,0.4, π/2} .
While a representative proximal initialization within this regime (C0 = 0.4) matches the high fidelity
of exact inversion under posterior sampling (G= 0.008 ), it preserves measurement-consistent struc-
tures even in unconditional sampling, unlike the unrelated output from random initialization.

**中文:**

**图 2：近端初始化与采样随机性的交互作用（高斯去模糊）。** (a) 在不同累积随机性 $G$ 下，PSNR 与 LPIPS 作为扰动尺度 $C_0$ 的函数，说明存在一个充分性区间（本设置中 $C_0 \le 0.4$），在该区间内近端初始化的表现与精确反演相当。(b) 当 $C_0 \in \{0, 0.4, \pi/2\}$ 时的视觉对比。尽管该区间内一个有代表性的近端初始化（$C_0 = 0.4$）在后验采样（$G = 0.008$）下能匹配精确反演的高保真度，但即便在无条件采样下它仍保留与观测一致的结构，而随机初始化给出的是无关输出。

bound. Achieving sufficient proximity thus controls the initialization term adequately without ex-

<span id="S005-04"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-04

**Original:**

where C0 ∈[0, π/2] controls the perturbation scale. This construction approximately preserves the
Gaussian typical norm ∥˜x1∥2 ≈
√
d, providing a clean empirical counterpart to the initialization
error term in (4). Figure 2(a) visualizes the perception-distortion landscape as a joint function of
the perturbation scale C0, reflecting δ2 along the horizontal axis, and the cumulative stochasticity G
across the curve family.

**中文:**

其中 $C_0 \in [0, \pi/2]$ 控制扰动尺度。该构造近似保持高斯典型范数 $\|\tilde{x}_1\|_2 \approx \sqrt{d}$，为式 (4) 中的初始化误差项提供了一个清晰的经验对应物。图 2(a) 把感知–失真景观可视化为扰动尺度 $C_0$（横轴体现 $\delta^2$）与累积随机性 $G$（曲线族）的联合函数。

---

<span id="S005-05"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-05

**Original:**

Sufficiency of proximal source initialization.Across all G curves in Figure 2(a), reconstruction
quality remains nearly flat within an initial proximity range before it begins to degrade. The exact
extent of this flat region (here, up to C0 ≈0.4 ) depends on the specific setup. We refer to this
empirically observed region as the “sufficiency regime”. Within this regime, further reducing δ
provides negligible gain. Figure 2(b) qualitatively corroborates this. Under posterior sampling
(G= 0.008 ), a moderately proximal initialization from this regime (e.g., C0 = 0.4 ) achieves
reconstructions visually comparable to exact inversion ( C0 = 0 ), whereas random initialization
(C0 =π/2 ) incurs a substantial quality drop. Unconditional sampling further confirms that the
proximal noise encodes measurement-consistent structure, while the random noise produces an
unrelated image.

**中文:**

**近端源初始化的充分性。** 在图 2(a) 的所有 $G$ 曲线上，重建质量在一个初始临近度范围内几乎保持不变，随后才开始下降。这一平坦区间的确切范围（此处约为 $C_0 \approx 0.4$）取决于具体设置。我们把这一经验观察到的区域称为“充分性区间”。在该区间内，进一步减小 $\delta$ 带来的收益可以忽略。图 2(b) 从定性上印证了这一点。在后验采样（$G = 0.008$）下，取自该区间的一个适度近端初始化（例如 $C_0 = 0.4$）所获得的重建在视觉上与精确反演（$C_0 = 0$）相当，而随机初始化（$C_0 = \pi/2$）会带来明显的质量下降。无条件采样进一步证实近端噪声编码了与观测一致的结构，而随机噪声产生的是无关图像。

---

<span id="S005-06"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-06

**Original:**

Attenuation by excessive stochasticity.Turning to the curve family in Figure 2(a), increasing G
degrades reconstruction quality. This is qualitatively consistent with Proposition 3.1: as G grows, the
stochastic departure term d·G rises directly, so the bound increases regardless of how wellδ has been
reduced. Consequently, a well chosen initialization alone is insufficient. Bounding G throughout
guided sampling helps preserve the advantage conferred by proximal noise initialization. Additional
empirical validation is provided in Appendix B.

**中文:**

**过度随机性的衰减效应。** 回到图 2(a) 的曲线族，增大 $G$ 会降低重建质量。这与命题 3.1 定性一致：随着 $G$ 增大，随机偏离项 $d\cdot G$ 直接上升，因此无论 $\delta$ 被减小到何种程度，上界都会增大。因此，仅有一个选择良好的初始化并不充分。在引导采样全程约束 $G$ 有助于保持近端噪声初始化所赋予的优势。附录 B 给出了额外的经验验证。

---

<span id="S005-07"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-07

**Original:**

4 Method: The FLINT Framework

**中文:**

## 4 方法：FLINT 框架

---

<span id="S005-08"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-08

**Original:**

The analysis in Sec. 3 motivates the two-stage design of FLINT. Since exhaustive source space opti-
mization is redundant under the sufficiency regime (Sec. 3), Stage 1 (Sec. 4.1) efficiently anchors the
initial noise via a manifold constrained proximal objective with spectrally weighted data consistency.
Stage 2 (Sec. 4.2) then employs a bounded stochasticity schedule during guided posterior sampling,
preventing excessive stochasticity from eroding the fidelity attained by this initialization (Sec. 3).

**中文:**

第 3 节的分析启发了 FLINT 的两阶段设计。由于在充分性区间下穷尽式的源空间优化是冗余的（第 3 节），第 1 阶段（第 4.1 节）利用带谱加权数据一致性的流形约束近端目标函数，高效地锚定初始噪声。第 2 阶段（第 4.2 节）随后在引导后验采样过程中采用有界随机性调度，防止过度随机性侵蚀该初始化所达到的保真度（第 3 节）。

---

<span id="S005-09"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-09

**Original:**

4.1 Stage 1: Proximal Source Initialization

**中文:**

### 4.1 第 1 阶段：近端源初始化

---

<span id="S005-10"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-10

**Original:**

Manifold-constrained proximal objective.Unlike source space MAP methods that incur massive
computational costs for a deterministic exact solution, or MCMC approaches that exhaustively
characterize the source posterior distribution, Stage 1 focuses solely on rapidly attaining sufficient
proximity, a measurement-informed initialization statistically consistent with the generative prior. To
derive a principled objective, we employ a variational relaxation, minimizing the KL divergence from
a surrogate distributionq ϕ(x1) =N(x 1 |µ ϕ, ρ2I)to the intractable source posteriorp(x 1 |y):

**中文:**

**流形约束的近端目标函数。** 与那些为求确定性精确解而付出巨大计算代价的源空间 MAP 方法、或为穷尽刻画源后验分布而采用 MCMC 的方法不同，第 1 阶段只关注快速达到足够的临近度，即一个受观测信息影响、且与生成先验在统计上一致的初始化。为推导一个有依据的目标函数，我们采用变分松弛，最小化代理分布 $q_\phi(x_1) = \mathcal{N}(x_1 \mid \mu_\phi, \rho^2 I)$ 到难以处理的源后验 $p(x_1 \mid y)$ 的 KL 散度：

---

<span id="S005-11"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-11

**Original:**

min
ϕ
KL(qϕ(x1)∥p(x 1 |y))
=−E qϕ[logp(y|x 1)]| {z }
data fidelity
+ KL(qϕ(x1)∥p(x 1))| {z }
prior regularization
+ logp(y) (6)

**中文:**

$$\min_\phi\ \mathrm{KL}\big(q_\phi(x_1)\,\|\,p(x_1 \mid y)\big) = \underbrace{-\mathbb{E}_{q_\phi}\big[\log p(y \mid x_1)\big]}_{\text{数据保真}} + \underbrace{\mathrm{KL}\big(q_\phi(x_1)\,\|\,p(x_1)\big)}_{\text{先验正则}} + \log p(y) \tag{6}$$

**中文:**

**算法 1：用于基于流的反问题的 FLINT 求解器**

**第 1 阶段：源空间近端初始化**

**Require:** $y, A, v_\theta, \beta_{\mathrm{KL}}, \lambda_{\mathrm{cum}}$

1: 初始化 $\mu_\phi \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I)$
2: **for** $s = 1, \ldots, S$ **do**
3: $\quad x_1 \leftarrow \mu_\phi + \rho^{(s)}\epsilon$，$\epsilon \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I)$
4: $\quad \hat{x}_0 \leftarrow \hat{\Phi}_{\mathrm{tr}}(x_1)$ ▷ 算法 2
5: $\quad J \leftarrow \ell_{\mathrm{IR}}(y, \hat{x}_0) + \beta_{\mathrm{KL}}\,\mathrm{KL}\big(q_\phi(x_1)\,\|\,p(x_1)\big) + \lambda_{\mathrm{cum}}\,R(\mu_\phi)$ ▷ $\ell_{\mathrm{IR}}$：式 (8)
6: $\quad \mu_\phi \leftarrow \mathrm{Adam}(\nabla_{\mu_\phi} J)$
7: **end for**
8: **return** $\tilde{x}_1 = \mu_\phi$

**第 2 阶段：带约束随机性的保临近度后验采样**

**Require:** $\tilde{x}_1, y, A, v_\theta, \lambda, c, c_\emptyset, \eta(t)$ ▷ $\eta(t)$：式 (10)

1: $x_1 \leftarrow \tilde{x}_1$
2: **for** $t: 1 \to 0$ **do**
3: $\quad v_t \leftarrow v_\theta(x_t, c_\emptyset) + \lambda\big(v_\theta(x_t, c) - v_\theta(x_t, c_\emptyset)\big)$
4: $\quad \hat{x}_{0|t} \leftarrow x_t - \sigma_t v_t$，$\hat{x}_{1|t} \leftarrow x_t + (1 - \sigma_t)v_t$
5: $\quad \tilde{x}_{0|t} \leftarrow \arg\min_x \mathcal{L}\big(A(x), y\big)$ ▷ 以 $\hat{x}_{0|t}$ 初始化
6: $\quad \epsilon \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I)$
7: $\quad x_{t+\Delta t} \leftarrow (1 - \sigma_{t+\Delta t})\tilde{x}_{0|t} + \sigma_{t+\Delta t}\hat{x}_{1|t} + \eta(t)\epsilon$
8: **end for**
9: **return** $x_0$

<span id="S006-02"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S006-02

**Original:**

where logp(y) is constant. Crucially, the standard KL term only constrains the global norm of µϕ
but cannot preclude higher-order departures from Gaussianity in source space. To encourage the
optimized noise adheres to the prior manifold, we augment the objective with a higher order cumulant
regularizerR(µ ϕ)that penalizes non-Gaussian drift during optimization (Appendix C.1).
Combining this regularizer with the computationally efficient truncated samplingbΦtr (Sec. 2.3) and
the data consistency loss (Sec. 4.1), we formulate the manifold-constrained proximal objective:

**中文:**

其中 $\log p(y)$ 为常数。关键在于，标准 KL 项只约束 $\mu_\phi$ 的全局范数，无法排除源空间中偏离高斯性的高阶偏差。为促使优化后的噪声贴合先验流形，我们在目标中引入高阶累积量正则项 $R(\mu_\phi)$，在优化过程中惩罚非高斯漂移（附录 C.1）。把该正则项与计算高效的截断采样 $\hat{\Phi}_{\mathrm{tr}}$（第 2.3 节）以及数据一致性损失（第 4.1 节）相结合，我们构造出流形约束的近端目标函数：

---

<span id="S006-03"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S006-03

**Original:**

J(ϕ) =E x1∼qϕ
h
ℓIR(y, bΦtr(x1))
i
+β KL ·KL(q ϕ(x1)∥p(x 1)) +λ cum · R(µϕ),(7)

**中文:**

$$J(\phi) = \mathbb{E}_{x_1\sim q_\phi}\big[\ell_{\mathrm{IR}}(y, \hat{\Phi}_{\mathrm{tr}}(x_1))\big] + \beta_{\mathrm{KL}}\cdot\mathrm{KL}\big(q_\phi(x_1)\,\|\,p(x_1)\big) + \lambda_{\mathrm{cum}}\cdot R(\mu_\phi), \tag{7}$$

---

<span id="S006-04"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S006-04

**Original:**

where the expectation is approximated via a single sample Monte Carlo estimatorx1 =µ ϕ +ρϵ with
a fixed annealing schedule for ρ. Gradients of J(ϕ) are computed via the SDO shortcut combined
withbΦtr, yielding a low per-iteration cost. Within just a few iterations, this optimization yields a
proximity-initialized noise, a starting point residing in a low energy posterior region.

**中文:**

其中期望用单样本蒙特卡洛估计 $x_1 = \mu_\phi + \rho\epsilon$ 近似，$\rho$ 采用固定的退火调度。$J(\phi)$ 的梯度通过 SDO 捷径结合 $\hat{\Phi}_{\mathrm{tr}}$ 计算，每次迭代开销很低。仅需少数几次迭代，该优化便给出一个近端初始化的噪声，即位于低能量后验区域的起点。

---

<span id="S006-05"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S006-05

**Original:**

Spectrally weighted data consistency.The truncated ODE estimate ˆx0 =bΦtr(x1) is spectrally
biased: with few reverse sampling steps, high-frequency components remain largely unresolved [9],
making data consistency gradients unreliable over the full spectrum and destabilizing the proximal
optimization. We address this by estimating frequency dependent reliability with the Wiener filter
framework [63]. For the flow-matching interpolant xtstop = (1−t stop)x0 +t stopϵ, the signal and
noise powers at frequency ω are (1−t stop)2Sx0(ω) and t2
stop, respectively, where Sx0(ω)∝ ∥ω∥ −α
follows the well-established pink noise model for natural images [50]. Applying the Wiener filter
formula directly yields the following data consistency loss:

**中文:**

**谱加权数据一致性。** 截断 ODE 估计 $\hat{x}_0 = \hat{\Phi}_{\mathrm{tr}}(x_1)$ 存在谱偏置：当反向采样步数很少时，高频分量在很大程度上未被解析 [9]，使数据一致性梯度在整个频谱上不可靠，并破坏近端优化的稳定性。我们借助 Wiener 滤波框架 [63] 估计随频率变化的可靠性。对于 Flow Matching 的插值 $x_{t_{\mathrm{stop}}} = (1 - t_{\mathrm{stop}})x_0 + t_{\mathrm{stop}}\epsilon$，频率 $\omega$ 处的信号功率与噪声功率分别为 $(1 - t_{\mathrm{stop}})^2 S_{x_0}(\omega)$ 与 $t_{\mathrm{stop}}^2$，其中 $S_{x_0}(\omega) \propto \|\omega\|^{-\alpha}$ 遵循关于自然图像已被充分确立的粉噪模型 [50]。直接应用 Wiener 滤波公式，得到如下数据一致性损失：

---

<span id="S006-06"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S006-06

**Original:**

ℓIR(y, ˆx0) =∥W(t stop)⊙ F[A ∗(Aˆx0 −y)]∥ 2
2 , W(ω, t stop) = (1−t stop)2Sx0(ω)
(1−t stop)2Sx0(ω) +t 2
stop
, (8)

**中文:**

$$\ell_{\mathrm{IR}}(y, \hat{x}_0) = \big\|W(t_{\mathrm{stop}})\odot\mathcal{F}[A^*(A\hat{x}_0 - y)]\big\|_2^2,\qquad W(\omega, t_{\mathrm{stop}}) = \frac{(1 - t_{\mathrm{stop}})^2 S_{x_0}(\omega)}{(1 - t_{\mathrm{stop}})^2 S_{x_0}(\omega) + t_{\mathrm{stop}}^2}, \tag{8}$$

---

<span id="S006-07"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S006-07

**Original:**

Here, F and A∗ denotes the 2D Fourier transform, and the adjoint of the forward operator, respec-
tively. A∗(Aˆx0 −y) back-projects the measurement residual to the image domain before spectral
filtering. The weight W∈[0,1] emphasizes reliable low frequencies and decays toward 0 at high
frequencies. By suppressing gradients from unreliable frequency components, Eq. (8) provides stable
low-frequency supervision, yielding faster convergence than full-spectrum data consistency.

**中文:**

这里 $\mathcal{F}$ 与 $A^*$ 分别表示二维傅里叶变换与前向算子的伴随。$A^*(A\hat{x}_0 - y)$ 先把观测残差反投影到图像域，再进行谱滤波。权重 $W \in [0, 1]$ 强调可靠的频率分量并随频率升高衰减趋近 0，从而抑制来自不可靠频率分量的梯度；式 (8) 因而提供稳定的低频监督，相比全谱数据一致性收敛更快。

---

<span id="S006-08"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S006-08

**Original:**

4.2 Stage 2: Proximity-Preserving Posterior Sampling with Bounded Stochasticity

**中文:**

### 4.2 第 2 阶段：带约束随机性的保临近度后验采样

---

<span id="S006-09"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S006-09

**Original:**

Stage 2 starts from the proximity-initialized source ˜x1 =µ ϕ produced by Stage 1, which provides
a measurement-informed starting point. Its role is not to rediscover a measurement-consistent low-
frequency structure from scratch, but rather to perform the proximity-preserving stochastic refinement
along the sampling trajectory. We adopt the framework of the second sub-family of trajectory-space
methods described in Sec. 2.2. At each reverse step, this approach obtains a clean image estimate via
the flow-based Tweedie’s formula, applies a data consistency correction in the clean image space, and
renoises the corrected estimate onto the generative prior manifold at the next timestep. Concretely,
the next state is computed as:

**中文:**

第 2 阶段从第 1 阶段产生的近端初始化源 $\tilde{x}_1 = \mu_\phi$ 出发，它提供了一个受观测信息影响的起点。其作用不是从零重新发现与观测一致的低频结构，而是沿采样轨迹执行保持临近度的随机精化。我们采用第 2.2 节所述轨迹空间方法第二子类的框架。在每一步反向步中，该方法经基于流的 Tweedie 公式得到干净图像估计，在干净图像空间中施加数据一致性修正，并把修正后的估计重新加噪到下一个时间步的生成先验流形上。具体地，下一状态按下式计算：

---

<span id="S006-10"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S006-10

**Original:**

xt+∆t = (1−σ t+∆t) ˜x0|t(y) +σ t+∆t ˆx1|t +η(t)ϵ,ϵ∼ N(0,I),(9)

**中文:**

$$x_{t+\Delta t} = (1 - \sigma_{t+\Delta t})\tilde{x}_{0|t}(y) + \sigma_{t+\Delta t}\hat{x}_{1|t} + \eta(t)\epsilon,\quad \epsilon\sim\mathcal{N}(0,I), \tag{9}$$

---

<span id="S006-11"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S006-11

**Original:**

where σt denotes the noise level, and the update reinterpolates the corrected clean estimate ˜x0|t(y)
and noise estimate ˆx1|t back onto the(t+ ∆t)-manifold while injecting stochasticity scaleη(t).

**中文:**

其中 $\sigma_t$ 表示噪声水平，该更新把修正后的干净估计 $\tilde{x}_{0|t}(y)$ 与噪声估计 $\hat{x}_{1|t}$ 重新插值回 $(t + \Delta t)$ 流形，同时注入尺度为 $\eta(t)$ 的随机性。

### 表 1：在流匹配模型上的定量比较

---

<span id="S007-02"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-02

**Original:**

Bounded stochasticity schedule.Proposition 3.1 motivates limiting stochastic accumulation but
does not uniquely determine how the stochasticity budget should be allocated over time. We design
the following stochasticity schedule:

**中文:**

**有界随机性调度（Bounded stochasticity schedule）。** 命题 3.1 支持限制随机性累积，但并未唯一确定随机性预算应如何在时间上分配。我们设计如下随机性调度：

---

<span id="S007-03"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-03

**Original:**

η(t) =λ s ·
√
1−σ t ·σ t,(10)

**中文:**

$$\eta(t) = \lambda_s \cdot \sqrt{1 - \sigma_t}\cdot\sigma_t, \tag{10}$$

> 原抽取把根号与被开方式拆到多行，已还原。

---

<span id="S007-04"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-04

**Original:**

where λs controls the overall magnitude. The shape is deliberately chosen to endow Eq. (9) with a
proximity-preserving role. At early timesteps t≈1 (σt ≈1 ), η(t) remains close to zero, suppressing
stochastic perturbations during the phase where the coarse image layout is determined in flow-based
models. Large perturbations at this stage would erode the low-energy proximal state established
by Stage 1. As t decreases to 0, η(t) again decays to zero, effectively concentrating stochasticity
in the intermediate timesteps where perceptual details are refined without disturbing the global
structure. With λs <1 , the bell-shaped profile of η(t) keeps
R 1
0 η2(t) dt bounded, aiming to preserve
the initialization advantage throughout guided sampling. The overall algorithm is summarized in
Algorithm 1.

**中文:**

其中 $\lambda_s$ 控制整体幅度。该形状是被刻意选定的，目的是赋予式 (9) 以保临近度的作用。在早期时间步 $t \approx 1$（$\sigma_t \approx 1$）时，$\eta(t)$ 保持接近零，从而在基于流的模型确定图像粗略布局的阶段抑制随机扰动。该阶段的大扰动会侵蚀第 1 阶段建立的低能量近端状态。随着 $t$ 减小到 0，$\eta(t)$ 再次衰减到零，从而有效地把随机性集中到中间时间步——在这些时间步上精化感知细节而不扰动全局结构。当 $\lambda_s < 1$ 时，$\eta(t)$ 的钟形轮廓使 $\int_0^1 \eta^2(t)\,dt$ 有界，旨在在引导采样全程保持初始化优势。整体算法总结于算法 1。

---

<span id="S007-05"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-05

**Original:**

5 Experiments

**中文:**

## 5 实验

---

<span id="S007-06"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-06

**Original:**

5.1 Experimental Setup

**中文:**

### 5.1 实验设置

---

<span id="S007-07"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-07

**Original:**

Datasets and metrics.Experiments are conducted on FFHQ [ 24] ( 10242, 1k samples) and
DIV2K [1] (2K, 800 samples), with PSNR, SSIM [ 61], LPIPS [ 68], and patch-based FID [ 20]
(256×256 patches, InceptionV3) as evaluation metrics. Additionally, for ablation studies conducted
on a smaller subset (100 samples), we report patch-based KID [6].

**中文:**

**数据集与评价指标。** 实验在 FFHQ [24]（$1024^2$，1,000 个样本）与 DIV2K [1]（2K，800 个样本）上进行，评价指标为 PSNR、SSIM [61]、LPIPS [68] 以及基于图块的 FID [20]（$256\times256$ 图块，InceptionV3）。此外，对于在更小子集（100 个样本）上进行的消融实验，我们报告基于图块的 KID [6]。

---

<span id="S007-08"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-08

**Original:**

Baselines.We compare against recent flow matching approaches: FlowChef [ 41], FlowDPS [26],
FLAIR [17], Flower [42], and FlowLPS [40], all built upon Stable Diffusion 3.5-Medium (7682, NFE
50). For FLINT, the same 50 NFE budget is split into 21 NFEs for Stage 1 (K=3 truncated steps ×7
iterations) and 29 NFEs for Stage 2. Baseline configurations are detailed in Appendix E.

**中文:**

**基线。** 我们与近期流匹配方法比较：FlowChef [41]、FlowDPS [26]、FLAIR [17]、Flower [42] 与 FlowLPS [40]，它们均构建在 Stable Diffusion 3.5-Medium（$768^2$，NFE 50）之上。对于 FLINT，同样的 50 次 NFE 预算被划分为第 1 阶段 21 次 NFE（$K = 3$ 个截断步 × 7 次迭代）与第 2 阶段 29 次 NFE。基线配置详见附录 E。

---

<span id="S007-09"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-09

**Original:**

Problem setting.We evaluate on several linear inverse problems: SR ×8, ×12, motion deblurring
with a 61×61 kernel at intensity 0.5, and Gaussian deblurring with a 61×61 kernel at standard
deviation 3.0. All tasks incorporate additive Gaussian noise with σy = 0.03 (corresponding to 1.5%
of the normalized pixel range). For text conditioning, a fixed prompt ("A high quality photo of a
face") is used for FFHQ, while image adaptive captions from DAPE [64] are used for DIV2K.

**中文:**

**问题设置。** 我们在若干线性反问题上评估：SR ×8、×12，核尺寸 $61\times61$、强度 0.5 的运动去模糊，以及核尺寸 $61\times61$、标准差 3.0 的高斯去模糊。所有任务都加入 $\sigma_y = 0.03$ 的加性高斯噪声（对应归一化像素范围的 1.5%）。文本条件方面，FFHQ 使用固定提示词（“A high quality photo of a face”），DIV2K 使用来自 DAPE [64] 的图像自适应描述。

---

<span id="S007-10"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-10

**Original:**

5.2 Experimental Results

**中文:**

### 5.2 实验结果

---

<span id="S007-11"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-11

**Original:**

Flow matching model.As shown in Table 1, FLINT consistently outperforms all flow matching
baselines, yielding favorable results across both distortion and perceptual quality metrics. Fig. 3
corroborates these results, with structural artifacts of baseline reconstructions visibly suppressed. Ex-
tended evaluations on diffusion model backbones (Stable Diffusion 1.5) are provided in Appendix D.

**中文:**

**流匹配模型。** 如表 1 所示，FLINT 一致优于所有流匹配基线，在失真与感知质量指标上均取得有利结果。图 3 印证了这些结果，基线重建中的结构性伪影被明显抑制。在扩散模型骨干（Stable Diffusion 1.5）上的扩展评估见附录 D。

Computational cost and performance.Fig. 4 evaluates SR ×8 and Gaussian deblurring on 1000
FFHQ samples, varying Stage 2 over {4,9,19,29} steps (total budgets {25,30,40,50} ). With as
few as 30 NFEs, FLINT surpasses nearly all baselines at the full 50 NFE budget on both PSNR and
LPIPS. This advantage stems from the proximal initialization. Stage 1 requires only a small number
of optimization iterations at low cost enabled by Sec. 2.3, which reduces the computational burden of
Stage 2 by positioning the initial noise in a favorable region of the source space, requiring only a

**中文:**

**计算开销与性能。** 图 4 在 1,000 张 FFHQ 样本上评估 SR ×8 与高斯去模糊，把第 2 阶段的步数在 $\{4, 9, 19, 29\}$ 之间变化（总预算 $\{25, 30, 40, 50\}$）。仅用 30 次 NFE，FLINT 在 PSNR 与 LPIPS 上就几乎超过所有基线在完整 50 次 NFE 预算下的表现。这一优势源于近端初始化：第 1 阶段只需少量低成本优化迭代（由第 2.3 节支撑），它通过把初始噪声置于源空间的有利区域而降低了第 2 阶段的计算负担，只需

<span id="S009-05"></span>
**Source:** p.9 S009-05

**Original:**

Diffusion model.To assess applicability beyond flow matching, we further evaluate FLINT on
Stable Diffusion 1.5 (5122, 200 NFEs) against PSLD [46], ReSample [51], DDPG [19], and TReg [28].
As shown in Table 2, FLINT achieves the best PSNR, FID, and LPIPS on SR×4 and motion deblurring,
as well as the best SSIM on the latter task. Detailed settings and qualitative comparisons are provided
in Appendix D, with baseline configurations in Appendix E.2.

**中文:**

**扩散模型。** 为评估在流匹配之外的可适用性，我们进一步在 Stable Diffusion 1.5（$512^2$，200 次 NFE）上把 FLINT 与 PSLD [46]、ReSample [51]、DDPG [19] 和 TReg [28] 比较。如表 2 所示，FLINT 在 SR×4 与运动去模糊上取得最佳 PSNR、FID 与 LPIPS，并在后一任务上取得最佳 SSIM。详细设置与定性比较见附录 D，基线配置见附录 E.2。

---

<span id="S009-06"></span>
**Source:** p.9 S009-06

**Original:**

5.3 Ablation Studies

**中文:**

### 5.3 消融实验

---

<span id="S009-07"></span>
**Source:** p.9 S009-07

**Original:**

Effectiveness of stage 1: MCPO and SWDC.Table 3a isolates the contribution of each component
within Stage 1, the Manifold-Constrained Proximal Objective (MCPO) and Spectrally Weighted
Data Consistency (SWDC). Comparing Conf. A (random) against Conf. C (full proximal) confirms
that the posterior sampling started from proximal-initialized noise yields substantial gains across all
metrics. Figure 5 visually corroborates this: unconditional sampling from the proximal noise recovers
a coarse structure consistent with the measurement, whereas the same procedure from a random
Gaussian draw produces an entirely unrelated image. Incorporating the SWDC (B to C) improves
reconstruction quality by suppressing gradients from unreliable high-frequency components during
the optimization, ensuring stable convergence.

**中文:**

**第 1 阶段的有效性：MCPO 与 SWDC。** 表 3a 分离出第 1 阶段中各组件的贡献，即流形约束近端目标函数（Manifold-Constrained Proximal Objective, MCPO）与谱加权数据一致性（Spectrally Weighted Data Consistency, SWDC）。比较配置 A（随机）与配置 C（完整近端）可确认：从近端初始化噪声出发的后验采样在所有指标上都带来显著增益。图 5 从视觉上印证了这一点：从近端噪声做无条件采样可恢复与观测一致的粗略结构，而对随机高斯抽样执行同样流程则产生完全无关的图像。加入 SWDC（从 B 到 C）可在优化过程中抑制来自不可靠高频分量的梯度，从而提升重建质量并保证稳定收敛。

---

<span id="S009-08"></span>
**Source:** p.9 S009-08

**Original:**

Sensitivity to the bounded stochasticity schedule (BSS).Table 3b ablates the stochasticity
schedule along two axes: the magnitude λs and the temporal shape ofη(t), comparing our bell-shaped
schedule (Eq. 10) against a front-loaded alternative ηmono(t) =λ s
p
1−(1−σ t)2 σt that decreases
monotonically over t: 1→0 and peaks at t= 1 . Two key trends emerge. (i) Magnitude: While
Sec. 3 warns against excessive stochasticity (largeλs ≥1.3 ) due to the amplified stochastic departure,
suppressing it (λs →0.3 ) limits the FLINT’s ability to perform stochastic exploration concentrated
at middle timesteps, resulting in suboptimal performance. The moderate setting (λs = 0.8) achieves
the optimal balance, bounded to preserve the low-energy proximal state yet active enough to achieve
a high-fidelity reconstruction. (ii) Shape: at matched λs, the bell-shaped schedule generally yields
superior performance, particularly as λs increases, as front loading disrupts the proximal noise
precisely when coarse structure is being established.

**中文:**

**对有界随机性调度（BSS）的敏感性。** 表 3b 沿两个维度消融随机性调度：幅度 $\lambda_s$ 与 $\eta(t)$ 的时间形状，并把我们钟形调度（式 10）与一个前加载（front-loaded）替代方案 $\eta_{\mathrm{mono}}(t) = \lambda_s\sqrt{1 - (1 - \sigma_t)^2}\,\sigma_t$ 比较，后者随 $t: 1 \to 0$ 单调递减并在 $t = 1$ 处达到峰值。由此出现两个关键趋势。(i) 幅度：第 3 节警示过度随机性（较大的 $\lambda_s \ge 1.3$）会因随机偏离被放大而有害；但过度抑制（$\lambda_s \to 0.3$）又会限制 FLINT 在中间时间步集中进行随机探索的能力，导致性能次优。适度设置（$\lambda_s = 0.8$）取得最优平衡：既受约束以保持低能量近端状态，又足够活跃以获得高保真重建。(ii) 形状：在相同 $\lambda_s$ 下，钟形调度总体性能更优，尤其在 $\lambda_s$ 增大时，因为前加载恰好在粗略结构正在建立之时破坏了近端噪声。

**Source:** p.10 S010-C1

**Original:**

Figure 6:Quantitative comparison of the sam-
pling processvia Tweedie’s estimates.

**中文:**

**图 6：通过 Tweedie 估计对采样过程的定量比较。**

---

<span id="S010-03"></span>
**Source:** p.10 S010-03

**Original:**

The theoretical and empirical findings in Sec. 3
predict that proximal initialization reduces the
initialization gap, while bounded stochasticity
preserves this advantage throughout guided sam-
pling. Fig. 6 corroborates this prediction by eval-
uating the reconstruction quality of Tweedie’s
estimate along the sampling trajectory (visual-
izations in Appendix F.3). FLINT enters the
measurement-consistent subspace earlier than
baselines and sustains higher reconstruction
quality through convergence. The Appendix
further provides extensive analyses on the effect of the cumulant regularizer (Appendix F.4), com-
parisons with alternative initialization strategies including forward noising initialization [ 36] and
flow inversion in both deterministic [15] and stochastic variants [45] (Appendix F.5), the effect of
prolonged source space optimization (Appendix F.6), and a posterior variance analysis confirming
the absence of mode collapse (Appendix F.7).

**中文:**

第 3 节的理论与经验发现预言：近端初始化会缩小初始化差距，而有界随机性在整个引导采样过程中保持这一优势。图 6 通过评估采样轨迹上 Tweedie 估计的重建质量印证了这一预言（可视化见附录 F.3）。FLINT 比基线更早进入与观测一致的子空间，并在收敛过程中保持更高的重建质量。附录还提供了大量分析：累积量正则项的影响（附录 F.4）；与替代初始化策略的比较，包括前向加噪初始化 [36] 以及确定性 [15] 与随机 [45] 两种流反演变体（附录 F.5）；延长源空间优化的效果（附录 F.6）；以及确认不存在模式崩塌的后验方差分析（附录 F.7）。

---

<span id="S010-04"></span>
**Source:** p.10 S010-04

**Original:**

6 Conclusion

**中文:**

## 6 结论

---

<span id="S010-05"></span>
**Source:** p.10 S010-05

**Original:**

We introduce FLINT, a two-stage framework that bridges source-space and trajectory-space meth-
ods by exploiting their coupling, offering a new design principle for flow-based inverse solvers.
Through a unified stability analysis, we motivate coupling proximal source initialization with bounded
stochasticity to preserve the initialization advantage during posterior sampling. FLINT employs a
manifold-constrained proximal objective followed by a strategic stochasticity schedule, consistently
improving reconstruction quality across diverse inverse problems at a competitive sampling cost.

**中文:**

我们提出 FLINT，一个通过利用源空间方法与轨迹空间方法之间的耦合来桥接二者的两阶段框架，为基于流的反问题求解器提供了新的设计原则。通过统一的稳定性分析，我们论证了把近端源初始化与有界随机性耦合起来、以在后验采样过程中保持初始化优势的动机。FLINT 采用流形约束的近端目标函数，随后接一个策略性的随机性调度，在可比的采样开销下一致地提升多种反问题的重建质量。

---

<span id="S010-06"></span>
**Source:** p.10 S010-06

**Original:**

Limitation.FLINT inherits the limitations of the underlying pretrained generative models, including
training data biases and reduced capacity for out-of-distribution images. Reconstruction quality may
degrade on scenes with high structural complexity (e.g., multiple objects or instances), where the
increased ill-posedness makes it difficult to simultaneously satisfy data fidelity and prior consistency.

**中文:**

**局限性。** FLINT 继承了底层预训练生成模型的固有局限，包括训练数据偏置以及对分布外图像处理能力下降。在结构复杂度高的场景（例如包含多个物体或多个实例）中，重建质量可能下降——此类场景的不适定性更强，难以同时满足数据保真与先验一致性。

---

<span id="S014-04"></span>

**Original:**

This appendix formalizes the interpretation introduced in Remark 1 (Section 3): when the reference
point x∗
1 in Proposition 3.1 is taken to be the posterior energy minimizer, source-space proximity
controls a likelihood energy upper bound, while a random Gaussian draw typically lies farther away
in source space. A principled initialization refers to an initial noise placed deliberately close to the
source-space solution, as opposed to a random draw from the prior. We define the posterior energy as
the negative log-posterior up to a constant:

**中文:**

本附录把注 1（第 3 节）引入的解释形式化：当命题 3.1 中的参考点 $x_1^*$ 取为后验能量的极小值点时，源空间临近度控制的是一个似然能量的上界，而随机高斯抽样通常位于源空间中更远的位置。所谓“有依据的初始化”，指的是刻意放在源空间解附近的初始噪声，而非从先验中随机抽样。我们把后验能量定义为负对数后验（相差一个常数）：

<span id="S016-01"></span>
**Source:** p.16 S016-01

**Original:**

Remark 2(Comparison with two-sided Lipschitz).A standard two-sided Lipschitz constant bounds
the operator norm of the full Jacobian, which is always nonnegative. The one-sided constant above
bounds to the maximum eigenvalue of the symmetric part of the Jacobian, which can benegative.
In particular, log-concavity of pt(·|y) allows a nonpositive choice of ms(t), in which case the score
term contributes a nonpositive term to the effective contraction rate. This can reduce the prefactor in
our bound by offsetting expansion from the posterior velocity.

**中文:**

**注 2（与双侧 Lipschitz 的比较）。** 标准的双侧 Lipschitz 常数界定的是整个雅可比算子的算子范数，而算子范数总是非负的。上述单边常数界定的是雅可比对称部分的最大特征值，它可以是负的。特别地，$p_t(\cdot \mid y)$ 的对数凹性允许 $m_s(t)$ 取非正值，此时得分项对有效收缩率贡献一个非正项。这可以通过抵消来自后验速度的扩张来减小我们界中的前因子。

<span id="S017-12"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-12

**Original:**

B Additional Empirical Validation: Erosion by Excessive Stochasticity

**中文:**

## B 额外经验验证：过度随机性造成的侵蚀

---

<span id="S017-13"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-13

**Original:**

This section provides additional empirical validation for the erosion phenomenon identified in
Section 3, complementing the joint analysis of Figure 2(a) with a controlled experiment that isolates
the effect of exessive cumulative stochasticity from the initialization gap. We fix the initialization at
either proximal (C0 = 0.4) or random (C0 =π/2 ) and vary the cumulative stochasticity G. Under
this design, Figure S1(a) shows that at low G, proximal initialization substantially outperforms
random initialization across both PSNR and LPIPS, confirming that a well-chosen starting point
confers a meaningful reconstruction advantage when cumulative stochasticity is bounded. AsG grows,
the performance gap narrows and eventually vanishes. The direction of this trend agrees with the
monotone dependence of the bound in Proposition 3.1 onG, although the bound itself does not predict
that the gap closes. Figure S1(b) corroborates this qualitatively. At low stochasticity (G= 0.008 ),
the proximal initialization produces reconstructions that are perceptually sharper and more faithful
to the ground truth than the random initialization, demonstrating that the initialization advantage
translates into visible quality differences. At high stochasticity ( G= 0.12 ), both initialization
types yield reconstructions of comparable and degraded quality, visually confirming that excessive
cumulative noise injection overrides any benefit from a principled starting point. Together, these
results suggest that bounding G plays a key role in preserving the initialization advantage throughout
guided sampling.

**中文:**

本节为第 3 节所识别的侵蚀现象提供额外经验验证：通过一个受控实验把过度累积随机性的影响与初始化差距隔离开来，从而补充图 2(a) 的联合分析。我们把初始化固定为近端（$C_0 = 0.4$）或随机（$C_0 = \pi/2$），并改变累积随机性 $G$。在该设计下，图 S1(a) 表明：在 $G$ 较低时，近端初始化在 PSNR 与 LPIPS 上都明显优于随机初始化，这证实当累积随机性有界时，一个选择良好的起点能带来有意义的重建优势。随着 $G$ 增大，性能差距缩小并最终消失。该趋势的方向与命题 3.1 中界对 $G$ 的单调依赖一致，尽管该界本身并未预言差距会闭合。图 S1(b) 从定性上证认了这一点。在低随机性（$G = 0.008$）下，近端初始化产生的重建在感知上比随机初始化更清晰、也更接近真值，说明初始化优势会转化为可见的质量差异。在高随机性（$G = 0.12$）下，两种初始化都给出质量相近且已退化的重建，从视觉上证实过度累积的噪声注入会盖过任何有依据起点带来的收益。综上，这些结果表明：在引导采样全程约束 $G$ 对保持初始化优势起关键作用。

> 原文 “exessive” 为 excessive 的拼写错误，译文按 excessive 理解。

---

## C FLINT 算法的更多细节

<span id="S017-15"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-15

**Original:**

C.1 Stage 1: Proximal Source Initialization

**中文:**

### C.1 第 1 阶段：近端源初始化

---

<span id="S017-16"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-16

**Original:**

We cast source-space initialization as variational optimization by introducing a variational distribution
qϕ(x1) =N(x 1 |µ ϕ, ρ2Id) over the initial noise and minimizing the objective in Eq.(7) of the main
paper. The expectation in Eq. (7) is approximated by a single reparameterized sample per iteration,
i.e., x1 =µ ϕ +ρε , ε∼ N(0,I d). We find this sufficient in practice given the smoothness ofℓIR
over the spectrally filtered subspace 4.1. Gradients ∇ϕJ(ϕ) are computed via the SDO shortcut
combined with the truncated ODE forward pass.

**中文:**

通过引入初始噪声上的变分分布 $q_\phi(x_1) = \mathcal{N}(x_1 \mid \mu_\phi, \rho^2 I_d)$ 并最小化正文式 (7) 中的目标，我们把源空间初始化表述为变分优化。式 (7) 中的期望在每次迭代中由单个重参数化样本近似，即 $x_1 = \mu_\phi + \rho\epsilon$、$\epsilon \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I_d)$。考虑到 $\ell_{\mathrm{IR}}$ 在谱滤波子空间（第 4.1 节）上的光滑性，我们发现这在实践中已足够。梯度 $\nabla_\phi J(\phi)$ 通过 SDO 捷径结合截断 ODE 前向传播计算。

---

**Source:** p.17 S017-17

**Original:**

divergence admits a closed form. For twod-dimensional Gaussians with diagonal covariance,

**中文:**

**闭式 KL 项。** 由于 $q_\phi$ 与先验 $p(x_1) = \mathcal{N}(0, I_d)$ 都是高斯的，KL 散度存在闭式表达。对于两个具有对角协方差的 $d$ 维高斯，

---

**Source:** p.17 S017-18

**Original:**

KL

= 1
2
h
tr(ρ2Id) +µ ⊤I−1
d µ−d+ log det(Id)
det(ρ2Id)
i
,(S16)

$$\mathrm{KL}\Big[\mathcal{N}(\mu, \rho^2 I_d)\,\Big\|\, \mathcal{N}(0, I_d)\Big] = \frac{1}{2}\Big[\mathrm{tr}(\rho^2 I_d) + \mu^\top I_d^{-1}\mu - d + \log\frac{\det(I_d)}{\det(\rho^2 I_d)}\Big], \tag{S16}$$

Figure S1: Additional empirical validation for erosion by excessive stochasticity. (a) PSNR and
LPIPS as a function of the cumulative stochasticity G for proximal initialization ( C0 = 0.4) and
random initialization (C0 =π/2 ). Proximal initialization outperforms random initialization at low G,
while the performance gap narrows as G increases. (b) Qualitative comparison across initialization
types (proximal vs. random, rows) and stochasticity levels (high G= 0.12 vs. low G= 0.008 ,
columns).

**中文:**

**图 S1：过度随机性造成侵蚀的额外经验验证。** (a) 近端初始化（$C_0 = 0.4$）与随机初始化（$C_0 = \pi/2$）下，PSNR 与 LPIPS 作为累积随机性 $G$ 的函数。在低 $G$ 时近端初始化优于随机初始化，而随着 $G$ 增大性能差距收窄。(b) 不同初始化类型（近端 vs. 随机，行）与不同随机性水平（高 $G = 0.12$ vs. 低 $G = 0.008$，列）下的定性对比。

Closed-form cumulant regularizer.The KL term alone cannot preclude higher-order departures
from the Gaussian prior, for instance, a heavy-tailed or skewed coordinate distribution that shares
the first two moments of N(0,1) . To address this, we include the cumulant regularizer R(µϕ),
which penalizes non-zero standardized skewness and excess kurtosis of the per-channel coordinate
distribution of µϕ. Treating the HW spatial coordinates of channel c as an empirical sample, define
the per-channel empirical mean and variance:

**中文:**

<span id="S019-01"></span>

κ2

48 to KL(p∥N(0,1)) [35]. Consequently, R constitutes a second-order approximation to the KL
divergence between the empirical marginal coordinate distribution of each channel and N(0,1) .
Together, the KL term controls the norm ofµϕ, while R suppresses skewness and excess kurtosis,
jointly reducing the risk of the optimized noise drifting outside the training support of the pretrained
flow model during optimization.

**中文:**

$\kappa_4^2/48$ 对 $\mathrm{KL}(p \,\|\, \mathcal{N}(0,1))$ 的贡献 [35]。（承接第 18 页末句）因此，$R$ 构成各通道经验边缘坐标分布与 $\mathcal{N}(0,1)$ 之间 KL 散度的一个二阶近似。综合来看，KL 项控制 $\mu_\phi$ 的范数，而 $R$ 抑制偏度与超额峰度，二者共同降低优化后的噪声在优化过程中漂出预训练流模型训练支撑集的风险。

Noise annealing schedule.The varianceρis linearly annealed overKoptimization iterations:

**中文:**

**噪声退火调度。** 方差 $\rho$ 在 $K$ 次优化迭代中线性退火：

ρ(s) =ρ max − s
S (ρmax −ρ min), s= 0,1, . . . , S,(S22)

<span id="S019-04"></span>

with ρmin = 0.02 . Large ρ in early iterations smooths the optimization landscape and prevents
premature convergence to suboptimal µϕ. As ρ decays toward ρmin, gradient estimates concentrate
aroundµ ϕ, enabling fine-grained refinement within the identified low-energy region.

**中文:**

其中 $\rho_{\min} = 0.02$。早期迭代中较大的 $\rho$ 会平滑优化地形，防止过早收敛到次优的 $\mu_\phi$。随着 $\rho$ 衰减到 $\rho_{\min}$，梯度估计集中到 $\mu_\phi$ 附近，从而可在已识别的低能量区域内进行细粒度精化。

**Original:**

At the start of Stage 2, the sample ˜x1 =µ ϕ produced by Stage 1 replaces the standard random
initialization, positioning the sampler within a low posterior energy region from the outset. Each
reverse step proceeds through four sequential operations.

**中文:**

在第 2 阶段开始时，第 1 阶段产生的样本 $\tilde{x}_1 = \mu_\phi$ 取代标准的随机初始化，使采样器从一开始就位于低后验能量区域。每一步反向步依次经过四个操作。

Text-conditional velocity field.At each timestep t, the pretrained flow model is queried under the
text conditioncwith a fixed classifier-free guidance scaleλ:

**中文:**

**文本条件速度场。** 在每个时间步 $t$，在文本条件 $c$ 下以固定的无分类器引导尺度 $\lambda$ 查询预训练流模型：

**Original:**

vt(xt) =v θ(xt, c∅) +λ
vθ(xt, c)−v θ(xt, c∅)
.(S23)

$$v_t(x_t) = v_\theta(x_t, c_\emptyset) + \lambda\big(v_\theta(x_t, c) - v_\theta(x_t, c_\emptyset)\big). \tag{S23}$$

<span id="S019-09"></span>

**Original:**

Decomposition via Tweedie’s formula.The velocity vt(xt) simultaneously yields a clean image
estimate ˆx0|t and a source noise estimate ˆx1|t via the flow-based Tweedie’s formula:

**中文:**

**经由 Tweedie 公式分解。** 速度 $v_t(x_t)$ 同时通过基于流的 Tweedie 公式给出干净图像估计 $\hat{x}_{0|t}$ 与源噪声估计 $\hat{x}_{1|t}$：

ˆx0|t =x t −σ t vt(xt), ˆx1|t =x t + (1−σ t)v t(xt),(S24)

$$\hat{x}_{0|t} = x_t - \sigma_t v_t(x_t),\qquad \hat{x}_{1|t} = x_t + (1 - \sigma_t)v_t(x_t), \tag{S24}$$

---

<span id="S019-11"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S019-11

**Original:**

whereσ t denotes the noise schedule.

其中 $\sigma_t$ 表示噪声调度。

where L measures the discrepancy between the forward projection of the current estimate and the
observed measurement, and β(t) is a time-varying data consistency guidance scale. The loss L in

Least-Squares (LS) objective [19]:

**中文:**

其中 $\mathcal{L}$ 度量当前估计的前向投影与观测测量之间的差异，$\beta(t)$ 是随时间变化的数据一致性引导尺度。式 (S25) 中的损失 $\mathcal{L}$ 实例化为反投影（Back-Projection, BP）目标与最小二乘（Least-Squares, LS）目标的加权插值 [19]：

$$\mathcal{L}\big(\mathcal{A}(\hat{x}_{0|t}), y\big) = (1 - \omega_t)\big\|\mathcal{A}^\dagger(y) - \mathcal{A}^\dagger\big(\mathcal{A}(\hat{x}_{0|t})\big)\big\|^2 + \omega_t\big\|y - \mathcal{A}(\hat{x}_{0|t})\big\|^2, \tag{S26}$$

where A† is the pseudo-inverse of A. The interpolation weight ωt = (1−σ ω(t))ϕ with ϕ= 0.8

t→0. For flow matching models,σ ω(t) =σ t. For diffusion models,σ ω(t) = 1−¯αt.

其中 $\mathcal{A}^\dagger$ 是 $\mathcal{A}$ 的伪逆。插值权重 $\omega_t = (1 - \sigma_\omega(t))^\phi$、$\phi = 0.8$，使修正在高噪声水平下以 BP 为主过渡到 $t \to 0$ 时以 LS 为主。对 Flow Matching 模型，$\sigma_\omega(t) = \sigma_t$；对扩散模型，$\sigma_\omega(t) = 1 - \bar{\alpha}_t$。

Euler update with bounded stochasticity.To preserve the initialization advantage from Stage 1,
the next state is obtained by renoising the corrected clean estimate ˜x0|t(y) to the (t+ ∆t) -manifold
with stochasticity injected into the source noise component under the bounded scheduleη(t):

**中文:**

**带约束随机性的 Euler 更新。** 为保持第 1 阶段的初始化优势，下一状态由把修正后的干净估计 $\tilde{x}_{0|t}(y)$ 重新加噪到 $(t + \Delta t)$ 流形得到，其中随机性在有界调度 $\eta(t)$ 下注入源噪声分量：

xt+∆t = (1−σ t+∆t) ˜x0|t(y) +σ t+∆t ˆx1|t +η(t)ϵ,ϵ∼ N(0,I).(S27)

**中文:**

$$x_{t+\Delta t} = (1 - \sigma_{t+\Delta t})\tilde{x}_{0|t}(y) + \sigma_{t+\Delta t}\hat{x}_{1|t} + \eta(t)\epsilon,\quad \epsilon \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I). \tag{S27}$$

## PDF 第 20 页

<span id="S020-01"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-01

**Original:**

Algorithm 2Truncated Sampling with SDO Shortcut
Require:x 1,v θ,(c, c ∅),λ,K
1:x←x 1
2:fori= 1, . . . , K−1do
3:v←v θ(x, c∅) +λ(v θ(x, c)−v θ(x, c∅))
4:ifi̸= 1then
5:v←stop_grad(v)▷SDO shortcut: retain graph only ati= 1
6:end if
7:x←x+ (τ i+1 −τ i)v▷Euler update;τ i =τ (N)
i
8:end for
9:t stop ←τ K−1
10:v tstop ←stop_grad(v θ(x, c ∅) +λ(v θ(x, c)−v θ(x, c ∅)))
11: ˆx0 ←x−t stop ·v tstop ▷Tweedie’s formula (Eq. (2))
12:return ˆx0

**中文:**

**算法 2：带 SDO 捷径的截断采样**

**Require:** $x_1, v_\theta, (c, c_\emptyset), \lambda, K$

1: $x \leftarrow x_1$
2: **for** $i = 1, \ldots, K-1$ **do**
3: $\quad v \leftarrow v_\theta(x, c_\emptyset) + \lambda\big(v_\theta(x, c) - v_\theta(x, c_\emptyset)\big)$
4: $\quad$ **if** $i \neq 1$ **then**
5: $\quad\quad v \leftarrow \mathrm{stop\_grad}(v)$ ▷ SDO 捷径：仅在 $i = 1$ 处保留计算图
6: $\quad$ **end if**
7: $\quad x \leftarrow x + (\tau_{i+1} - \tau_i)v$ ▷ Euler 更新；$\tau_i = \tau_i^{(N)}$
8: **end for**
9: $t_{\mathrm{stop}} \leftarrow \tau_{K-1}$
10: $v_{t_{\mathrm{stop}}} \leftarrow \mathrm{stop\_grad}\big(v_\theta(x, c_\emptyset) + \lambda(v_\theta(x, c) - v_\theta(x, c_\emptyset))\big)$
11: $\hat{x}_0 \leftarrow x - t_{\mathrm{stop}}\cdot v_{t_{\mathrm{stop}}}$ ▷ Tweedie 公式（式 (2)）
12: **return** $\hat{x}_0$

> 伪代码标识符保留原文；原抽取把 “stop_grad” 与注释压平，已按语义还原缩进。

---

<span id="S020-03"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-03

**Original:**

We evaluate our approach against diffusion based baselines implemented on Stable Diffusion 1.5
(5122, NFE 200), encompassing PSLD [46], ReSample [51], DDPG [19], and TReg [28]. Hyperpa-
rameters for each baseline are provided in E.2.

**中文:**

我们与在 Stable Diffusion 1.5（$512^2$，NFE 200）上实现的扩散基线比较，包括 PSLD [46]、ReSample [51]、DDPG [19] 与 TReg [28]。各基线的超参数见 E.2。

---

<span id="S020-04"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-04

**Original:**

D.3 Problem Setting

**中文:**

### D.3 问题设置

---

<span id="S020-05"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-05

**Original:**

Diffusion based experiments cover two inverse problems: super-resolution 4× and motion deblurring
with a kernel size of 61 and intensity of 0.5. Across all settings, Gaussian noise with standard
deviation of 0.03 (1.5% of the pixel range) is added to the measurements.

**中文:**

基于扩散的实验覆盖两个反问题：4× 超分辨率，以及核尺寸 61、强度 0.5 的运动去模糊。在所有设置中，观测都加入标准差 0.03（像素范围的 1.5%）的高斯噪声。

---

<span id="S020-06"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-06

**Original:**

D.4 Experimental Results for Diffusion Model

**中文:**

### D.4 扩散模型上的实验结果

---

<span id="S020-07"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-07

**Original:**

To evaluate the generalizability of FLINT beyond flow matching frameworks, we extend the proposed
method to diffusion models and benchmark it against diffusion based baselines on super resolution
×4 and motion deblurring. As summarized in Table 2, FLINT achieves the best distortion perception
trade-off across both tasks, demonstrating that proximal initialization via variational framework
and guided posterior sampling with a bounded stochasticity schedule generalize effectively to the
diffusion model setting. The qualitative comparisons in Fig. S2 corroborate these findings, with
FLINT producing reconstructions of higher visual fidelity and structural coherence than competing
baselines. Collectively, these results confirm that the core principles of FLINT are not specific to
rectified flow priors but transfer robustly across distinct generative mechanisms.

**中文:**

为评估 FLINT 在流匹配框架之外的泛化性，我们把所提方法扩展到扩散模型，并在 4× 超分辨率与运动去模糊上与扩散基线对比。如表 2 总结，FLINT 在两个任务上都取得最佳的失真–感知折衷，表明经变分框架的近端初始化与带约束随机性调度的引导后验采样可以有效地推广到扩散模型设置。图 S2 的定性比较印证了这些发现：FLINT 产生的重建在视觉保真度与结构一致性上优于竞争基线。综合来看，这些结果确认 FLINT 的核心原理并非整流流先验所特有，而是能稳健地迁移到不同的生成机制。

---

<span id="S020-08"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-08

**Original:**

E Implementation Details

**中文:**

## E 实现细节

---

<span id="S020-09"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-09

**Original:**

E.1 Implementation Details for FLINT

**中文:**

### E.1 FLINT 的实现细节

---

<span id="S020-10"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-10

**Original:**

E.1.1 Stage 1: Proximal Source Initialization

**中文:**

#### E.1.1 第 1 阶段：近端源初始化

<span id="S020-11"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-11

**Original:**

Optimization setup.The optimization parameters µϕ are optimized using the Adam optimizer.
The regularization coefficients are set to βKL = 0.1 and λcum = 10 for FFHQ under the flow
matching framework, with the learning rate ( αlr) set to 0.06. The noise annealing schedule uses
σmax = 0.5 and σmin = 0.02 throughout all flow matching experiments. Hyperparameters for
DIV2K and diffusion model experiments are summarized in Table S1.

**中文:**

**优化设置。** 优化参数 $\mu_\phi$ 用 Adam 优化器优化。在流匹配框架下针对 FFHQ，正则系数设为 $\beta_{\mathrm{KL}} = 0.1$、$\lambda_{\mathrm{cum}} = 10$，学习率（$\alpha_{\mathrm{lr}}$）设为 0.06。噪声退火调度在所有流匹配实验中取 $\sigma_{\max} = 0.5$、$\sigma_{\min} = 0.02$。DIV2K 与扩散模型实验的超参数汇总于表 S1。

---

<span id="S020-12"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-12

**Original:**

Truncated sampling configuration.For flow matching experiments, we use K= 3 truncated ODE
steps per optimization iteration, with7 optimization iterations in total, yieldingNFEStage1 =K×7 =
21 function evaluations. The remaining 29 NFEs are allocated to Stage 2 guided posterior sampling,
for a total budget of 50 NFEs. For diffusion model experiments, we use K= 10 truncated ODE steps
per optimization iteration with 10 optimization iterations, yielding NFEStage1 =K×10 = 100
function evaluations, with the remaining100NFEs allocated to Stage 2, for a total of200NFEs.

**中文:**

**截断采样配置。** 对流匹配实验，每次优化迭代使用 $K = 3$ 个截断 ODE 步、共 7 次优化迭代，因此 $\mathrm{NFE}_{\mathrm{Stage1}} = K \times 7 = 21$ 次函数评估。其余 29 次 NFE 分配给第 2 阶段的引导后验采样，总预算为 50 次 NFE。对扩散模型实验，每次优化迭代使用 $K = 10$ 个截断 ODE 步、共 10 次优化迭代，因此 $\mathrm{NFE}_{\mathrm{Stage1}} = K \times 10 = 100$ 次函数评估，其余 100 次 NFE 分配给第 2 阶段，总计 200 次 NFE。

<span id="S021-03"></span>
**Source:** p.21 S021-03

**Original:**

Calibration for spectrally weighted data consistency.The Wiener filter weight W(ω, t stop) in
Eq. 8 assumes a pink noise prior Sx0(ω)∝ ∥ω∥ −α for natural images [ 50], consistent with the
1/f2 power spectrum, with α= 2.0 and tstop = 0.976 fixed across all tasks and datasets. While α
determines the spectral shape of the prior, it leaves the proportionality constant undetermined, causing
the transition frequency between near unity and near zero mask values to shift in an uncontrolled
manner. Calibration resolves this ambiguity by rescaling the raw mask to attain a prescribed value
Wat_cutoff at a designated reference frequency ωc, thereby anchoring the transition between reliable
and unreliable frequency components as a half power point. We uniformly adopt ωc = 0.05 ,
Wat_cutoff = 0.5, and α= 2.0 across all tasks and datasets without task specific tuning, indicating that
the calibration scheme is robust to variations in degradation type and image domain.

**中文:**

**频谱加权数据一致性的标定。** 式 8 中的维纳滤波权重 $W(\omega, t_{stop})$ 假设自然图像服从粉红噪声先验 $S_{x_0}(\omega) \propto \|\omega\|^{-\alpha}$ [50]，与 $1/f^2$ 功率谱一致，其中 $\alpha = 2.0$、$t_{stop} = 0.976$ 在所有任务与数据集上固定不变。$\alpha$ 决定了先验的频谱形状，却使比例常数无法确定，从而导致掩膜值在接近 1 与接近 0 之间的过渡频率发生不受控的偏移。标定通过重缩放原始掩膜，使其在指定的参考频率 $\omega_c$ 处达到规定值 $W_{\text{at\_cutoff}}$，从而把可靠频率分量与不可靠频率分量之间的过渡锚定为半功率点。我们在所有任务与数据集上统一采用 $\omega_c = 0.05$、$W_{\text{at\_cutoff}} = 0.5$、$\alpha = 2.0$，无需针对具体任务调参，这表明该标定方案对退化类型与图像域的变化具有鲁棒性。

---

<span id="S021-04"></span>
**Source:** p.21 S021-04

**Original:**

E.1.2 Stage 2: Proximity-Preserving Posterior Sampling with Bounded Stochasticity

**中文:**

### E.1.2 第二阶段：具有有界随机性的近端保持后验采样

<span id="S021-05"></span>
**Source:** p.21 S021-05

**Original:**

Stochasticity magnitude.The overall magnitude λs of the bounded stochasticity schedule η(t)
in Eq. 10 governs the total amount of noise injected throughout the guided sampling trajectory and
is likewise task-dependent. A complete specification of the λs values used across all datasets and
inverse problems is provided in Table S2.

**中文:**

**随机性强度。** 有界随机性调度 $\eta(t)$（式 10）的整体强度 $\lambda_s$ 决定了整个引导采样轨迹中注入噪声的总量，且同样依赖于任务。表 S2 给出了所有数据集与反问题中所用 $\lambda_s$ 取值的完整说明。

<span id="S022-01"></span>

**Original:**

Data consistency guidance scale.The time-varying guidance scale β(t) in Eq. S25 controls the
step size of the measurement correction at each reverse timestep and is task-dependent. Specifically,
β(t) follows a monotonically decreasing schedule interpolating between a minimum value βmin and
a maximum value βmax over the sampling trajectory. The complete set of (βmin, βmax) values used
across all datasets and inverse problems is provided in Table S3.

**中文:**

**数据一致性引导尺度。** 式 S25 中随时间变化的引导尺度 $\beta(t)$ 控制每个反向时间步上测量校正的步长，并依赖于任务。具体而言，$\beta(t)$ 沿采样轨迹在最小值 $\beta_{min}$ 与最大值 $\beta_{max}$ 之间按单调递减的调度插值。表 S3 给出了所有数据集与反问题中使用的 $(\beta_{min}, \beta_{max})$ 取值的完整集合。

E.2 Implementation Details for Baseline Hyperparameter Settings

**中文:**

### E.2 基线超参数设置的实现细节

---

<span id="S022-05"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-05

**Original:**

Flow Matching Model BaselinesFor all flow matching evaluations, we utilize Stable Diffusion
3.5-Medium as the generative backbone with the time scheduler shift factor set to 4.0 and the number
of function evaluations fixed at 50. All experiments are conducted on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU.
The specific configurations for each method are as follows:

**中文:**

**流匹配模型基线。** 在所有流匹配评估中，我们采用 Stable Diffusion 3.5-Medium 作为生成主干，时间调度器偏移因子设为 4.0，函数评估次数固定为 50。所有实验均在单块 NVIDIA A100 GPU 上完成。各方法的具体配置如下：

---

<span id="S022-06"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-06

**Original:**

• FlowChefThe number of gradient descent steps and the step size serving as the data
consistency guidance scale are set to 3 and 1, respectively, via grid search on the calibration
set. The CFG scale is fixed at 2.0.

**中文:**

**FlowChef**：作为数据一致性引导尺度的梯度下降步数与步长，通过在校准集上的网格搜索分别设为 3 和 1。CFG 尺度固定为 2.0。

---

<span id="S022-07"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-07

**Original:**

• FlowDPSFlowDPS is evaluated in its standard configuration with 50 NFE and a CFG scale
of 2, using a step size of 15 with 3 gradient descent steps for the inpainting task and a step
size of 10 with 3 gradient descent steps for all remaining tasks.

**中文:**

**FlowDPS**：FlowDPS 按其标准配置评估，采用 50 NFE 与 CFG 尺度 2；图像修复任务使用步长 15 与 3 步梯度下降，其余任务使用步长 10 与 3 步梯度下降。

---

<span id="S022-08"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-08

**Original:**

• FLAIRWe employ 15 gradient descent steps for data consistency optimization with a CFG
scale of 2.0 in its standard configuration. The learning rate is set to 0.1 for deblurring tasks,
consistent with the original paper. For SR ×8 and SR ×12 tasks, the optimal learning rates
are determined via grid search on the calibration set: 2 for both tasks on FFHQ, 3 for SR ×8
on DIV2K, and 6 for SR×12on DIV2K.

**中文:**

**FLAIR**：我们采用 15 步梯度下降进行数据一致性优化，CFG 尺度为 2.0，即其标准配置。去模糊任务的学习率设为 0.1，与原论文一致。对于 SR ×8 与 SR ×12 任务，最优学习率通过在校准集上的网格搜索确定：FFHQ 上两项任务均为 2，DIV2K 上 SR ×8 为 3，DIV2K 上 SR ×12 为 6。

---

<span id="S022-09"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-09

**Original:**

• FlowerWe employ 15 steps of gradient descent with a step size of 15 for data consistency
optimization via a grid search on the calibration set. Consistent with the original authors,
the optional uncertainty term is found to destabilize restoration, and thus γ is set to 0 with
the CFG scale fixed at 2.0.

**中文:**

**Flower**：我们通过在校准集上的网格搜索，采用 15 步梯度下降、步长 15 进行数据一致性优化。与原作者的设置一致，可选的方差项被发现会使复原过程失稳，因此 $\gamma$ 设为 0，CFG 尺度固定为 2.0。

---

<span id="S022-10"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-10

**Original:**

• FlowLPSWe employ 6 steps of gradient descent with a step size of 20 for data consistency
optimization via a grid search on the calibration set. Consistent with the original paper, we
use a fixed number of Langevin-Proximal update steps, Np = 15, and a CFG scale of 2.0. A
constant step size ζt = 10−4 is used for Langevin dynamics across all timesteps. For the
proximal optimization, we utilized the SGD optimizer. We use an initial learning rate of
0.5 for super-resolution and 0.1 for other tasks. To ensure stability during optimization, we
apply a learning rate decay schedule: for super-resolution, the learning rate is decayed by a
factor of 0.85 every 5 iterations, and for inpainting, by 0.65 every 10 iterations.

**中文:**

**FlowLPS**：我们通过在校准集上的网格搜索，采用 6 步梯度下降、步长 20 进行数据一致性优化。与原论文一致，我们使用固定数量的 Langevin-近端更新步数 $N_p = 15$，CFG 尺度为 2.0。Langevin 动力学在所有时间步上使用常数步长 $\zeta_t = 10^{-4}$，近端优化采用 SGD 优化器。超分辨率任务的初始学习率为 0.5，其他任务为 0.1。为保证优化过程中的稳定性，我们采用学习率衰减调度：超分辨率任务每 5 次迭代将学习率衰减为原来的 0.85 倍，图像修复任务每 10 次迭代衰减为原来的 0.65 倍。

---

<span id="S022-11"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-11

**Original:**

• FLINTWe employ 6 steps of gradient descent for the data consistency update by default,
increasing to 9 for DIV2K motion deblurring and to 12 for DIV2K SR×12, with the CFG
scale fixed at 1.0. Remaining implementation details follow the setup described in Sec. E.1.

**中文:**

**FLINT**：默认情况下，我们对数据一致性更新采用 6 步梯度下降，DIV2K 运动去模糊增至 9 步，DIV2K SR×12 增至 12 步，CFG 尺度固定为 1.0。其余实现细节遵循第 E.1 节所述的设置。

## PDF 第 23 页

<span id="S023-01"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-01

**Original:**

Diffusion Model BaselinesFor all diffusion-based evaluations, we utilize Stable Diffusion 1.5
as the generative backbone with the NFE fixed at 100. To ensure a competitive comparison, hyper-
parameters for all baselines are determined via an extensive grid search on a calibration set. All
experiments are conducted on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU. Specific configurations for each method
are as follows:

**中文:**

**扩散模型基线。** 在所有基于扩散的评估中，我们采用 Stable Diffusion 1.5 作为生成主干，NFE 固定为 100。为确保对比具有竞争力，所有基线的超参数均通过在校准集上进行大量网格搜索确定。所有实验均在单块 NVIDIA A100 GPU 上完成。各方法的具体配置如下：

---

<span id="S023-02"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-02

**Original:**

• PSLDWe use the paper’s default loss weights ω= 1.0 (measurement fidelity) and γ= 0.1
(gluing), applied identically to both super-resolution and motion deblurring. Following the
official implementation, the gluing gradient is added to the DDIM update with no additional
step size, and A⊤ approximates the pseudo-inverse for all tasks. We disable classifier-free
guidance, following PSLD’s official setup with an empty null prompt.

**中文:**

**PSLD**：我们采用论文默认的损失权重 $\omega = 1.0$（测量保真）与 $\gamma = 0.1$（胶合，gluing），并同样应用于超分辨率与运动去模糊。遵循官方实现，胶合梯度直接加到 DDIM 更新上，不另加步长，且 $A^\top$ 对所有任务近似为伪逆。按照 PSLD 的官方设置，我们使用空（null）提示并禁用无分类器引导。

---

<span id="S023-03"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-03

**Original:**

• ResampleWe adopt the resampling hyperparameter γ
(
1−¯αt−1
¯αt
)(
1− ¯αt
¯αt−1
)
with γ= 40
as proposed in the original paper. To enforce hard data consistency, the skip step size is set
to 1, accompanied by 10 steps of gradient descent for SR×4 task and 20 steps of gradient
descent for motion deblurring task with a step size of 30. The CFG scale is fixed at 1.0.

**中文:**

**Resample**：我们采用原论文提出的重采样超参数 $\gamma\,\frac{1-\bar{\alpha}_{t-1}}{\bar{\alpha}_t}\,\frac{1-\bar{\alpha}_t}{\bar{\alpha}_{t-1}}$，其中 $\gamma = 40$。为强制满足硬数据一致性，跳过步长设为 1，并伴随梯度下降：SR ×4 任务为 10 步、运动去模糊任务为 20 步，梯度下降步长均为 30。CFG 尺度固定为 1.0。

> 原式中的圆括号在抽取文本中以控制字符（U+0010/U+0011）表示，且分式按分子、分母分行排列；此处将控制字符还原为圆括号，并在译文中按语义重建为单个行内公式。

---

<span id="S023-04"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-04

**Original:**

• DDPGWe set hyperparameters for SR ×4 and motion deblurring set to {γ, ζ,˜η}=
{10.0,0.8,0.3} and {5.0,0.6,0.6} , respectively, with the step size µt set to the theoret-
ically safe value µ∗
t . Here, γ controls the guidance transition from back-projection to
least-squares, ζ balances stochastic noise injection against reconstruction accuracy, and ˜η
regularizes the back-projection operator to prevent noise amplification.

**中文:**

**DDPG**：我们将 SR ×4 与运动去模糊的超参数分别设为 $\{\gamma, \zeta, \tilde{\eta}\} = \{10.0, 0.8, 0.3\}$ 与 $\{5.0, 0.6, 0.6\}$，步长 $\mu_t$ 设为理论安全值 $\mu_t^*$。其中 $\gamma$ 控制从反投影到最小二乘的引导过渡，$\zeta$ 平衡随机噪声注入与重建精度，$\tilde{\eta}$ 对反投影算子进行正则化以防止噪声放大。

---

<span id="S023-05"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-05

**Original:**

• TRegWe follow TReg’s algorithmic pipeline (CG-based data-consistency every third
sampling step within the first 170 of 200 DDIM steps, corresponding to t≳145 on the
standard schedule; adaptive negation of the null prompt; no DPS branch). The numerical
hyperparameters {CFG scale, λcg,lr null}={2.0,0.1,10 −5} are calibrated for our setting
(FFHQ×SD1.5×σ y = 0.03, SR×4and motion deblurring).

**中文:**

**TReg**：我们遵循 TReg 的算法流程（在前 170 个 DDIM 步内每第三个采样步执行一次基于 CG 的数据一致性，对应标准调度下 $t \gtrsim 145$；对空提示做自适应否定；不使用 DPS 分支）。数值超参数 $\{\text{CFG scale}, \lambda_{cg}, \mathrm{lr}_{null}\} = \{2.0, 0.1, 10^{-5}\}$ 针对我们的实验设置（FFHQ × SD1.5 × $\sigma_y = 0.03$，SR ×4 与运动去模糊）进行了校准。

---

<span id="S023-06"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-06

**Original:**

• FLINTWe employ 8 steps of gradient descent for the data consistency update for super-
resolution ×4 and 6 steps for motion deblurring, with the CFG scale fixed at 1.0. Remaining
implementation details follow the setup described in Sec. E.1.

**中文:**

**FLINT**：我们对超分辨率 ×4 的数据一致性更新采用 8 步梯度下降，运动去模糊采用 6 步，CFG 尺度固定为 1.0。其余实现细节遵循第 E.1 节所述的设置。

---

<span id="S023-07"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-07

**Original:**

F Additional Experiments

**中文:**

## F 附加实验

---

<span id="S023-08"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-08

**Original:**

F.1 Additional Task: Inpainting

**中文:**

### F.1 附加任务：图像修复

---

<span id="S023-09"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-09

**Original:**

Beyond the four inverse problems in Sec. 5.2, we evaluate FLINT on the inpainting task using a flow
matching model. As shown in Table S4 and Fig. S3, FLINT attains state of the art performance on
box inpainting across all metrics. Proximal initialization via variational framework combined with
guided posterior sampling under a bounded stochasticity schedule suppresses both boundary artifacts
and blurry textures prevalent in baseline posterior sampling approaches. Moreover, FLINT achieves a
favorable position on the perception–distortion trade-off, attaining competitive reconstruction fidelity
(PSNR, SSIM) while improving perceptual quality (LPIPS, FID).

**中文:**

在 5.2 节的四个反问题之外，我们还在使用流匹配模型的图像修复任务上评估 FLINT。如表 S4 与图 S3 所示，FLINT 在箱式修复（box inpainting）的所有指标上均达到当前最佳性能。通过变分框架进行近端初始化，并在有界随机性调度下进行引导后验采样，二者结合抑制了基线后验采样方法中普遍存在的边界伪影与模糊纹理。此外，FLINT 在感知—失真权衡上取得了有利位置，在获得有竞争力的重建保真度（PSNR、SSIM）的同时提升了感知质量（LPIPS、FID）。

<span id="S024-05"></span>
**Source:** p.24 S024-05

**Original:**

F.3 Additional Discussion 1: Qualitative Comparison of Sampling Process

**中文:**

### F.3 附加讨论 1：采样过程的定性比较

---

<span id="S024-06"></span>
**Source:** p.24 S024-06

**Original:**

Figure S4 visualizes the Tweedie’s estimate at representative intermediate timesteps for FLINT
and the competing baselines, complementing the quantitative trajectory analysis in Figure 6. At
early timesteps, the baseline Tweedie’s estimates remain dominated by noise and exhibit only weak
alignment with the underlying measurement, whereas FLINT’s estimate already reflects the coarse
structure of the target reconstruction. As sampling progresses, FLINT’s intermediate estimates
progressively refine perceptual detail without disturbing the coarse layout established at initialization,
whereas the baselines continue to form the coarse layout itself through much of the sampling
trajectory. These patterns visualize, in trajectory form, the two complementary mechanisms identified
in Section 3: proximal initialization places the sampler within a low-energy posterior region from the
outset, and the bounded stochasticity schedule preserves this advantage as detail-level refinement
proceeds. Together, they allow FLINT to converge along a noticeably shorter path through the
generative manifold than the baselines.

**中文:**

图 S4 可视化 FLINT 与竞争基线在若干代表性中间时间步上的 Tweedie 估计，对图 6 中的定量轨迹分析形成补充。在早期时间步，基线的 Tweedie 估计仍被噪声主导，与底层测量仅呈现弱对齐，而 FLINT 的估计已经体现出目标重建的粗略结构。随着采样推进，FLINT 的中间估计逐步细化感知细节，且不破坏初始化时已确立的粗略布局，而基线在采样轨迹的大部分阶段仍在自行构建粗略布局。这些现象以轨迹形式直观呈现了第 3 节所识别的两个互补机制：近端初始化使采样器从一开始就位于低能量后验区域，而有界随机性调度在细节级细化过程中保持这一优势。二者共同使 FLINT 沿生成流形上明显更短的路径收敛，而基线不具备这一性质。

---

---

<span id="S025-03"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S025-03

**Original:**

F.4 Additional Discussion 2: Effect of Cumulant Regularization

**中文:**

### F.4 附加讨论 2：累积量正则化的作用

---

<span id="S025-04"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S025-04

**Original:**

The cumulant regularizer R(µϕ) incorporated into the manifold-constrained proximal objective of
stage 1 serves to enforce strict adherence of the optimized noise to the statistical characteristics of the
Gaussian prior manifold. While the standard KL divergence primarily constrains lower-order statistics
such as mean and variance, this regularization term suppresses non-Gaussian drift by penalizing
departures in higher-order moments, including skewness and excess kurtosis. Table S6 shows that on
motion deblurring (FFHQ, 100 samples), incorporating this term improves perceptual quality metrics,
suggesting that controlling higher order statistics keeps the proximal initialization within the valid
input support of the pretrained generative model, enabling full utilization of proximity-preserving
posterior sampling of stage 2.

**中文:**

第一阶段在流形约束的近端目标中引入的累积量正则项 $R(\mu_\phi)$，用于强制优化后的噪声严格符合高斯先验流形的统计特性。标准 KL 散度主要约束均值与方差等低阶统计量，而该正则项通过惩罚包括偏度与超额峰度在内的高阶矩偏离来抑制非高斯漂移。表 S6 表明，在运动去模糊任务（FFHQ，100 个样本）上，加入该项可改善感知质量指标，说明控制高阶统计量可使近端初始化保持在预训练生成模型的有效输入支撑集内，从而充分利用第二阶段保持近端的后验采样。

---

<span id="S025-05"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S025-05

**Original:**

F.5 Additional Discussion 3: Comparison with Pseudo-Inverse-Based Initialization Strategies

**中文:**

### F.5 附加讨论 3：与基于伪逆的初始化策略的比较

---

<span id="S025-06"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S025-06

**Original:**

We compare FLINT against three initialization strategies that derive the starting noise from the pseudo-
inverse measurement, with Stage 2 fixed: forward-noising initialization [36, 13], which perturbs the
pseudo-inverse via a single linear interpolation; deterministic inversion [15], which integrates the
reverse generative ODE; and stochastic inversion [45], which integrates a controlled SDE biased
toward typical Gaussian noise. None of these approaches jointly satisfies the two proximal conditions:
proximity to a measurement-consistent region of source space and consistency with the prior typical
set.

**中文:**

我们将 FLINT 与三种从伪逆测量出发构造初始噪声的初始化策略进行比较（第二阶段固定不变）：前向加噪初始化 [36, 13]，通过一次线性插值扰动伪逆；确定性反演 [15]，对反向生成 ODE 积分；以及随机反演 [45]，对受控 SDE 积分并使其偏向典型高斯噪声。这些方法均不能同时满足两个近端条件：与源空间中测量一致区域的接近性，以及与先验典型集的一致性。

---

---

<span id="S026-02"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-02

**Original:**

The forward-noising path does not coincide
with the generative trajectory and satisfies nei-
ther condition. Deterministic inversion faith-
fully reproduces the pseudo-inverse but propa-
gates the spectral distortions of the degradation
operator into source space, yielding atypical
noise. Stochastic inversion enforces typicality
through its controller, yet the measurement op-
erator enters neither the control objective nor
the dynamics, causing the optimized noise to
drift toward a generic typical sample without
measurement awareness. FLINT’s variational formulation enforces both conditions jointly through a
closed-form prior-matching term and a data-fidelity term that explicitly involves the measurement
operator. Table S7 confirms this analysis, with FLINT attaining the best score across every metric.

**中文:**

前向加噪路径与生成轨迹并不重合，两个条件均不满足。确定性反演能忠实地复现伪逆，但会把退化算子的频谱畸变传播到源空间，产生非典型噪声。随机反演通过其控制器强制典型性，然而测量算子既未进入控制目标也未进入动力学，导致优化后的噪声在缺乏测量感知的情况下漂向一般的典型样本。FLINT 的变分表述通过闭式先验匹配项与显式包含测量算子的数据保真项联合施加这两个条件。表 S7 证实了这一分析：FLINT 在每项指标上都取得最佳得分。

---

<span id="S026-03"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-03

**Original:**

F.6 Additional Discussion 4: Excessive Source-Space Optimization

**中文:**

### F.6 附加讨论 4：过度的源空间优化

---

<span id="S026-04"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-04

**Original:**

This section examines the effect of prolonged source-space optimization on reconstruction quality,
corroborating the proximal-level optimization budget adopted in Stage 1. Excessive source-space
optimization pushes the initial noise out-of-distribution (OOD), reducing reconstruction quality.
To quantify this, we track the horizontal lag-1 spatial correlation of the optimized noise across
optimization steps. As shown in Figure S5(c), the correlation remains stable within the proximal
regime and gradually increases beyond it, eventually exceeding the 95% acceptance threshold for
i.i.d. N(0, I) , indicating that the optimized noise progressively drifts toward the boundary of the
prior’s typical set. This OOD drift manifests directly in reconstruction quality under guided posterior
sampling (Figure S5(a)). While prolonged optimization yields marginal fidelity gains (0.36 dB PSNR
from step 10 to 40), perceptual quality degrades substantially, with LPIPS increasing by over35%
(from 0.202 to 0.274), as the OOD noise can no longer be rendered into natural image structures
by the pretrained generative model. These results corroborate the proximal design choice of Stage
1. Prolonged source-space optimization beyond a proximal level yields limited fidelity gains while
reducing perceptual quality. A short proximal initialization is therefore preferable to exhaustive
source-space optimization.

**中文:**

本节考察长时间源空间优化对重建质量的影响，为第一阶段采用的近端级优化预算提供佐证。过度的源空间优化会把初始噪声推出分布（out-of-distribution，OOD），从而降低重建质量。为量化这一现象，我们跟踪优化后的噪声在各优化步上的水平滞后 1 空间相关。如图 S5(c) 所示，该相关性在近端区间内保持稳定，超出该区间后逐渐增大，最终超过 i.i.d. $N(0, I)$ 的 95% 接受阈值，表明优化后的噪声正逐步漂向先验典型集的边界。这种 OOD 漂移直接体现为引导后验采样下的重建质量变化（图 S5(a)）。虽然延长优化带来微小的保真度提升（从第 10 步到第 40 步 PSNR 提升 0.36 dB），但感知质量显著下降，LPIPS 上升超过 35%（从 0.202 增至 0.274），因为 OOD 噪声已无法被预训练生成模型渲染为自然图像结构。这些结果佐证了第一阶段采用近端设计的合理性：超出近端水平的长时间源空间优化只带来有限的保真度收益，却降低感知质量，因此较短的近端初始化优于穷尽式的源空间优化。

---

<span id="S026-C1"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-C1

**Original:**

noise can no longer be rendered into natural image structures by the pretrained generative model. (b)
PSNR and LPIPS of unconditional sampling rendered directly from the optimized noise (NFE = 29,
matched to Stage 2). (c) PSNR, LPIPS, and horizontal lag-1 spatial correlation of the optimized noise
on a representative sample. The correlation remains stable within the proximal regime and trends
upward beyond it, indicating a gradual drift toward the boundary of the prior’s typical set.

**中文:**

**图 S5：** 过度源空间优化对高斯去模糊任务（FFHQ，100 个样本）的影响。(a) FLINT 重建在不同噪声优化步数下的 PSNR 与 LPIPS。超出近端区间（10 步）后 PSNR 趋于平台，而 LPIPS 劣化超过 35%，因为 OOD 噪声已无法被预训练生成模型渲染为自然图像结构。(b) 直接由优化后噪声进行无条件采样所得结果的 PSNR 与 LPIPS（NFE = 29，与第二阶段匹配）。(c) 某代表性样本上优化后噪声的 PSNR、LPIPS 与水平滞后 1 空间相关。该相关性在近端区间内保持稳定，超出后呈上升趋势，表明噪声逐渐漂向先验典型集的边界。
