<span id="S026-06"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-06

**Original:**

To verify that FLINT does not suffer from mode collapse, we assess the posterior varianceVar[x|y]
for the super-resolution ×12 task by drawing 50 samples for a fixed observationy and computing their
pixel-wise standard deviation. Fig. S6 presents this analysis for FLINT alongside FLAIR, Flower,
FlowDPS, and FlowLPS. FLINT maintains meaningful posterior coverage with variance concentrated
in high-frequency texture regions, precisely the regions left underdetermined by the likelihood term
in the highly ill-posed task, confirming that FLINT does not degenerate into a single-mode solution.
This diversity arises from two complementary mechanisms. In Stage 1, the spectrally weighted

**中文:**

为验证 FLINT 不存在模式坍缩，我们针对超分辨率 ×12 任务评估后验方差 $\mathrm{Var}[x|y]$：对固定观测 $y$ 抽取 50 个样本并计算其逐像素标准差。图 S6 给出了 FLINT 与 FLAIR、Flower、FlowDPS、FlowLPS 的对比分析。FLINT 保持了有意义的后验覆盖，方差集中在高频纹理区域，而这正是该高度病态任务中似然项无法确定的区域，从而证实 FLINT 并未退化为单模态解。这种多样性源于两个互补机制。在第一阶段，频谱加权

<span id="page-27"></span>
## PDF 第 27 页

<span id="S027-01"></span>
**Source:** p.27 S027-01

**Original:**

data consistency loss anchors the low-frequency structure of the proximity-initialized noise to the
measurement while suppressing gradients from high-frequency components, leaving uncertainty in
fine-grained details. Combined with annealed noise in the variational optimization, this encourages
the optimizer to explore a range of measurement-consistent proximal points that share the same
coarse layout but differ in their high-frequency realization. In Stage 2, the bounded bell-shaped
stochasticity schedule concentrates noise injection in intermediate timesteps where perceptual detail
is refined, actively generating varying high-frequency components across samples while keeping
the low-frequency structure stable. Together, these results confirm that FLINT reliably explores the
posterior rather than collapsing to a single reconstruction.

**中文:**

数据一致性损失把接近初始化的噪声的低频结构锚定到测量上，同时抑制来自高频分量的梯度，从而在细粒度细节上保留不确定性。结合变分优化中的退火噪声，这促使优化器探索一系列测量一致的近端点，它们共享相同的粗略布局但在高频实现上各不相同。在第二阶段，有界钟形随机性调度把噪声注入集中在细化感知细节的中间时间步，在保持低频结构稳定的同时，主动在各样本间生成不同的高频分量。上述结果共同表明，FLINT 能够可靠地探索后验，而不会坍缩为单一重建。

Ground TruthFLINT
Ground TruthFLINT
Figure S11: Qualitative failure cases of FLINT on highly complex scenes (DIV2K dataset). Increased
ill-posedness in dense, multi-object images disrupts the balance between measurement fidelity and
the generative prior. This results in either hallucinated structures, where the model generates spurious
details to satisfy the prior, or blurry textures, where conflicting updates fail to resolve high-frequency
information. These cases highlight the inherited limitations of the pretrained backbone’s capacity for
complex out-of-distribution scenes.
32

**中文:**

**图 S11：** FLINT 在高度复杂场景（DIV2K 数据集）上的定性失败案例。密集、多目标图像中病态性的加剧破坏了测量保真度与生成先验之间的平衡。这导致两类结果：一类是“幻觉结构”——模型为满足先验而生成虚假细节；另一类是“模糊纹理”——相互冲突的更新无法解析高频信息。这些案例凸显了预训练主干对复杂分布外场景的固有能力局限。

图内标注：上、下两组均给出一组 Ground Truth（真值）与 FLINT 重建结果的并列对比。

NeurIPS Paper Checklist

**中文:**

## NeurIPS 论文检查清单

---

<span id="S033-02"></span>
**Source:** p.33 S033-02

**Original:**

1.Claims

**中文:**

### 1. 论断（Claims）

---

<span id="S033-03"></span>
**Source:** p.33 S033-03

**Original:**

Question: Do the main claims made in the abstract and introduction accurately reflect the
paper’s contributions and scope?
Answer: [Yes]
Justification: The Abstract and Introduction (Sec. 1) clearly reflect the paper’s contributions
and scope.

**中文:**

**问题：** 摘要与引言中提出的主要论断是否准确反映了论文的贡献与范围？

**回答：** [是]

**理由：** 摘要与引言（第 1 节）清晰地反映了论文的贡献与范围。

---

<span id="S033-04"></span>
**Source:** p.33 S033-04

**Original:**

Guidelines:
• The answer [N/A] means that the abstract and introduction do not include the claims
made in the paper.
• The abstract and/or introduction should clearly state the claims made, including the
contributions made in the paper and important assumptions and limitations. A [No] or
[N/A] answer to this question will not be perceived well by the reviewers.
• The claims made should match theoretical and experimental results, and reflect how
much the results can be expected to generalize to other settings.
• It is fine to include aspirational goals as motivation as long as it is clear that these goals
are not attained by the paper.

**中文:**

**指引：**

- 回答 [N/A] 表示摘要与引言未包含论文所提出的论断。
- 摘要和/或引言应清楚陈述论文提出的论断，包括论文所作的贡献以及重要假设与局限。对本问题回答 [No] 或 [N/A] 将不会得到审稿人的好评。
- 提出的论断应与理论与实验结果相符，并反映结果在多大程度上可推广到其他设置。
- 只要明确说明论文并未实现这些目标，将愿景性目标作为动机写入是允许的。

---

<span id="S033-05"></span>
**Source:** p.33 S033-05

**Original:**

2.Limitations

**中文:**

### 2. 局限性（Limitations）

---

<span id="S033-06"></span>
**Source:** p.33 S033-06

**Original:**

Question: Does the paper discuss the limitations of the work performed by the authors?
Answer: [Yes]
Justification: This paper discusses the limitations in Sec. 6.

**中文:**

**问题：** 论文是否讨论了作者所做工作的局限性？

**回答：** [是]

**理由：** 本文在第 6 节讨论了局限性。

---

<span id="S033-07"></span>
**Source:** p.33 S033-07

**Original:**

Guidelines:
• The answer [N/A] means that the paper has no limitation while the answer [No] means
that the paper has limitations, but those are not discussed in the paper.
• The authors are encouraged to create a separate “Limitations” section in their paper.
• The paper should point out any strong assumptions and how robust the results are to
violations of these assumptions (e.g., independence assumptions, noiseless settings,
model well-specification, asymptotic approximations only holding locally). The authors
should reflect on how these assumptions might be violated in practice and what the
implications would be.
• The authors should reflect on the scope of the claims made, e.g., if the approach was
only tested on a few datasets or with a few runs. In general, empirical results often
depend on implicit assumptions, which should be articulated.
• The authors should reflect on the factors that influence the performance of the approach.
For example, a facial recognition algorithm may perform poorly when image resolution
is low or images are taken in low lighting. Or a speech-to-text system might not be
used reliably to provide closed captions for online lectures because it fails to handle
technical jargon.
• The authors should discuss the computational efficiency of the proposed algorithms
and how they scale with dataset size.
• If applicable, the authors should discuss possible limitations of their approach to
address problems of privacy and fairness.
• While the authors might fear that complete honesty about limitations might be used by
reviewers as grounds for rejection, a worse outcome might be that reviewers discover
limitations that aren’t acknowledged in the paper. The authors should use their best
judgment and recognize that individual actions in favor of transparency play an impor-
tant role in developing norms that preserve the integrity of the community. Reviewers
will be specifically instructed to not penalize honesty concerning limitations.

**中文:**

**指引：**

- 回答 [N/A] 表示论文没有局限性；回答 [No] 表示论文存在局限性，但未在文中讨论。
- 鼓励作者在论文中单设“局限性”一节。
- 论文应指出任何强假设，以及结果对这些假设被违反时的鲁棒性（例如独立性假设、无噪设置、模型良好设定、仅在局部成立的渐近近似）。作者应思考这些假设在实践中可能如何被违反，以及由此产生的影响。
- 作者应思考所提论断的范围，例如该方法是否仅在少数数据集或少数次运行上测试过。一般而言，经验结果往往依赖隐含假设，这些假设应被明确表述。
- 作者应思考影响方法性能的因素。例如，人脸识别算法在图像分辨率较低或拍摄光线不足时可能表现不佳；语音转文字系统可能无法可靠地为在线讲座提供隐藏式字幕，因为它无法处理专业术语。
- 作者应讨论所提算法的计算效率以及其随数据集规模的扩展性。
- 在适用时，作者应讨论其方法在解决隐私与公平性问题方面可能存在的局限。
- 作者可能担心，完全坦诚地说明局限会被审稿人用作拒稿理由；但更糟的结果或许是审稿人发现了论文中未被承认的局限。作者应作出最佳判断，并认识到支持透明度的个体行动在形成维护社区诚信的规范中具有重要作用。审稿人将被明确要求不得因作者对局限的诚实说明而加以惩罚。

---

Guidelines:
• The answer [N/A] means that the paper does not include theoretical results.
• All the theorems, formulas, and proofs in the paper should be numbered and cross-
referenced.
• All assumptions should be clearly stated or referenced in the statement of any theorems.
• The proofs can either appear in the main paper or the supplemental material, but if
they appear in the supplemental material, the authors are encouraged to provide a short
proof sketch to provide intuition.
• Inversely, any informal proof provided in the core of the paper should be complemented
by formal proofs provided in appendix or supplemental material.
• Theorems and Lemmas that the proof relies upon should be properly referenced.

**中文:**

- 回答 [N/A] 表示论文不含理论结果。
- 论文中所有定理、公式与证明都应编号并可相互引用。
- 所有假设都应在各定理的陈述中清楚给出或引用。
- 证明可以放在主论文或补充材料中；若放在补充材料中，鼓励作者提供一个简短的证明概要，以便读者建立直觉。
- 反之，正文中给出的任何非形式证明都应由附录或补充材料中的形式证明加以补充。
- 证明所依赖的定理与引理应被恰当引用。

4.Experimental result reproducibility

**中文:**

### 4. 实验结果可复现性（Experimental result reproducibility）

---

<span id="S034-04"></span>
**Source:** p.34 S034-04

**Original:**

Question: Does the paper fully disclose all the information needed to reproduce the main ex-
perimental results of the paper to the extent that it affects the main claims and/or conclusions
of the paper (regardless of whether the code and data are provided or not)?
Answer: [Yes]
Justification: This paper provides sufficient information that can reproduce the results of the
main experiment in Sec. 5, Sec. E, and additional experiments in Sec. D, Sec. F.

**中文:**

**问题：** 论文是否充分披露了复现其主要实验结果所需的所有信息（就这些信息影响论文主要论断和/或结论的程度而言，无论是否提供代码与数据）？

**回答：** [是]

**理由：** 本文提供了足以复现第 5 节、第 E 节主要实验结果以及第 D 节、第 F 节附加实验结果的信息。

---

<span id="S034-05"></span>
**Source:** p.34 S034-05

**Original:**

Guidelines:
• The answer [N/A] means that the paper does not include experiments.
• If the paper includes experiments, a [No] answer to this question will not be perceived
well by the reviewers: Making the paper reproducible is important, regardless of
whether the code and data are provided or not.
• If the contribution is a dataset and/or model, the authors should describe the steps taken
to make their results reproducible or verifiable.
• Depending on the contribution, reproducibility can be accomplished in various ways.
For example, if the contribution is a novel architecture, describing the architecture fully
might suffice, or if the contribution is a specific model and empirical evaluation, it may
be necessary to either make it possible for others to replicate the model with the same
dataset, or provide access to the model. In general. releasing code and data is often
one good way to accomplish this, but reproducibility can also be provided via detailed
instructions for how to replicate the results, access to a hosted model (e.g., in the case
of a large language model), releasing of a model checkpoint, or other means that are
appropriate to the research performed.
• While NeurIPS does not require releasing code, the conference does require all submis-
sions to provide some reasonable avenue for reproducibility, which may depend on the
nature of the contribution. For example
(a) If the contribution is primarily a new algorithm, the paper should make it clear how
to reproduce that algorithm.
(b) If the contribution is primarily a new model architecture, the paper should describe
the architecture clearly and fully.
(c) If the contribution is a new model (e.g., a large language model), then there should
either be a way to access this model for reproducing the results or a way to reproduce
the model (e.g., with an open-source dataset or instructions for how to construct
the dataset).
(d) We recognize that reproducibility may be tricky in some cases, in which case
authors are welcome to describe the particular way they provide for reproducibility.
In the case of closed-source models, it may be that access to the model is limited in
some way (e.g., to registered users), but it should be possible for other researchers
to have some path to reproducing or verifying the results.

**中文:**

**指引：**

- 回答 [N/A] 表示论文不含实验。
- 若论文包含实验，对本问题回答 [No] 将不会得到审稿人的好评：无论是否提供代码与数据，使论文可复现都很重要。
- 若贡献是数据集和/或模型，作者应描述为使结果可复现或可验证所采取的步骤。
- 取决于贡献类型，可复现性可通过多种方式实现。例如，若贡献是新颖架构，完整描述该架构可能就已足够；若贡献是特定模型与经验评估，则可能需要让别人能用同一数据集复现该模型，或提供模型访问途径。一般而言，发布代码与数据通常是实现可复现性的良好方式之一，但也可通过详细说明如何复现结果、提供托管模型的访问（例如大语言模型的情形）、发布模型检查点，或其他适合该研究的方式来实现。
- 虽然 NeurIPS 不要求发布代码，但会议要求所有投稿都提供某种合理的可复现途径，具体形式可能取决于贡献的性质。例如：
  - (a) 若贡献主要是新算法，论文应清楚说明如何复现该算法。
  - (b) 若贡献主要是新模型架构，论文应清楚且完整地描述该架构。
  - (c) 若贡献是新模型（例如大语言模型），则应要么提供访问该模型以复现结果的途径，要么提供复现该模型的方法（例如开源数据集或构建数据集的说明）。
  - (d) 我们认识到在某些情况下可复现性可能较难实现，此时欢迎作者描述其提供可复现性的具体方式。对于闭源模型，模型访问可能受到某种限制（例如仅限注册用户），但仍应让其他研究者拥有一条复现或验证结果的途径。

<span id="S035-01"></span>

Question: Does the paper provide open access to the data and code, with sufficient instruc-
tions to faithfully reproduce the main experimental results, as described in supplemental
material?
Answer: [Yes]
Justification: We will release code and sample data to reproduce the experimental results in
Sec. 5 upon acceptance of the paper.

**中文:**

**问题：** 论文是否提供数据与代码的开放获取，并给出足以忠实复现主要实验结果的说明（如补充材料中所述）？

**回答：** [是]

**理由：** 论文被接收后，我们将发布代码与样本数据，以复现第 5 节的实验结果。

> 本块为第 34 页第 5 条目的问题与回答（标题在第 34 页末）。

<span id="S035-02"></span>
**Source:** p.35 S035-02

**Original:**

Guidelines:
• The answer [N/A] means that paper does not include experiments requiring code.
• Please see the NeurIPS code and data submission guidelines (https://neurips.cc/
public/guides/CodeSubmissionPolicy) for more details.
• While we encourage the release of code and data, we understand that this might not
be possible, so [No] is an acceptable answer. Papers cannot be rejected simply for not
including code, unless this is central to the contribution (e.g., for a new open-source
benchmark).
• The instructions should contain the exact command and environment needed to run to
reproduce the results. See the NeurIPS code and data submission guidelines (https:
//neurips.cc/public/guides/CodeSubmissionPolicy) for more details.
• The authors should provide instructions on data access and preparation, including how
to access the raw data, preprocessed data, intermediate data, and generated data, etc.
• The authors should provide scripts to reproduce all experimental results for the new
proposed method and baselines. If only a subset of experiments are reproducible, they
should state which ones are omitted from the script and why.
• At submission time, to preserve anonymity, the authors should release anonymized
versions (if applicable).
• Providing as much information as possible in supplemental material (appended to the
paper) is recommended, but including URLs to data and code is permitted.

**中文:**

**指引：**

- 回答 [N/A] 表示论文不含需要代码的实验。
- 更多细节请参见 NeurIPS 代码与数据提交指南（https://neurips.cc/public/guides/CodeSubmissionPolicy）。
- 虽然我们鼓励发布代码与数据，但也理解这可能无法做到，因此 [No] 是可接受的回答。论文不能仅因未包含代码而被拒，除非代码对其贡献至关重要（例如新的开源基准）。
- 说明应包含运行以复现结果所需的确切命令与环境。更多细节请参见 NeurIPS 代码与数据提交指南（https://neurips.cc/public/guides/CodeSubmissionPolicy）。
- 作者应提供数据访问与准备的说明，包括如何访问原始数据、预处理数据、中间数据以及生成数据等。
- 作者应提供复现新提出方法与基线全部实验结果的脚本。若只有部分实验可复现，应说明哪些实验未包含在脚本中以及原因。
- 投稿时，为保持匿名，作者应发布匿名化版本（如适用）。
- 建议在补充材料（附于论文之后）中提供尽可能多的信息，但允许包含数据与代码的 URL。

**中文:**

<span id="S035-08"></span>
**Source:** p.35 S035-08

**Original:**

Guidelines:
• The answer [N/A] means that the paper does not include experiments.
• The authors should answer [Yes] if the results are accompanied by error bars, confidence
intervals, or statistical significance tests, at least for the experiments that support the
main claims of the paper.
• The factors of variability that the error bars are capturing should be clearly stated (for
example, train/test split, initialization, random drawing of some parameter, or overall
run with given experimental conditions).

**中文:**

**指引：**

- 回答 [N/A] 表示论文不含实验。
- 若结果附带误差棒、置信区间或统计显著性检验（至少对支持论文主要论断的实验），作者应回答 [Yes]。
- 应清楚说明误差棒所反映的变异来源（例如训练/测试划分、初始化、某些参数的随机抽取，或给定实验条件下的整体运行）。

<span id="page-36"></span>
## PDF 第 36 页

<span id="S036-01"></span>
**Source:** p.36 S036-01

**Original:**

• The method for calculating the error bars should be explained (closed form formula,
call to a library function, bootstrap, etc.)
• The assumptions made should be given (e.g., Normally distributed errors).
• It should be clear whether the error bar is the standard deviation or the standard error
of the mean.
• It is OK to report 1-sigma error bars, but one should state it. The authors should
preferably report a 2-sigma error bar than state that they have a 96% CI, if the hypothesis
of Normality of errors is not verified.
• For asymmetric distributions, the authors should be careful not to show in tables or
figures symmetric error bars that would yield results that are out of range (e.g., negative
error rates).
• If error bars are reported in tables or plots, the authors should explain in the text how
they were calculated and reference the corresponding figures or tables in the text.

**中文:**

**指引（续）：**

- 误差棒的计算方法应加以说明（闭式公式、调用库函数、自助法等）。
- 应给出所作假设（例如误差服从正态分布）。
- 应明确误差棒表示的是标准差还是均值的标准误。
- 报告 1σ 误差棒是可以的，但应明确说明。若误差服从正态分布的假设未经验证，作者最好报告 2σ 误差棒，而不是声称具有 96% 置信区间。
- 对于非对称分布，作者应注意不要在表格或图中显示会给出超出范围结果（例如负的错误率）的对称误差棒。
- 若在表格或图中报告了误差棒，作者应在正文中说明其计算方式，并引用相应的图或表。

> 本块为第 35 页第 7 条目指引的续接部分。

[第 36 页](#page-36)

<span id="page-37"></span>
## PDF 第 37 页

<span id="S037-01"></span>
**Source:** p.37 S037-01

**Original:**

• If the authors answer [N/A] or [No], they should explain why their work has no societal
impact or why the paper does not address societal impact.
• Examples of negative societal impacts include potential malicious or unintended uses
(e.g., disinformation, generating fake profiles, surveillance), fairness considerations
(e.g., deployment of technologies that could make decisions that unfairly impact specific
groups), privacy considerations, and security considerations.
• The conference expects that many papers will be foundational research and not tied
to particular applications, let alone deployments. However, if there is a direct path to
any negative applications, the authors should point it out. For example, it is legitimate
to point out that an improvement in the quality of generative models could be used to
generate Deepfakes for disinformation. On the other hand, it is not needed to point out
that a generic algorithm for optimizing neural networks could enable people to train
models that generate Deepfakes faster.
• The authors should consider possible harms that could arise when the technology is
being used as intended and functioning correctly, harms that could arise when the
technology is being used as intended but gives incorrect results, and harms following
from (intentional or unintentional) misuse of the technology.
• If there are negative societal impacts, the authors could also discuss possible mitigation
strategies (e.g., gated release of models, providing defenses in addition to attacks,
mechanisms for monitoring misuse, mechanisms to monitor how a system learns from
feedback over time, improving the efficiency and accessibility of ML).

**中文:**

**指引（续）：**

- 若作者回答 [N/A] 或 [No]，应解释为什么本工作没有社会影响，或为什么论文未涉及社会影响。
- 负面社会影响的例子包括潜在的恶意或非预期使用（例如虚假信息、生成虚假个人资料、监控）、公平性考量（例如部署可能对特定群体产生不公平影响的技术）、隐私考量与安全考量。
- 会议要求许多论文属于基础研究，未必与具体应用甚至部署相关。然而，若存在通往任何负面应用的直接路径，作者应指出。例如，指出生成模型质量的提升可被用于生成用于虚假信息的 Deepfake 是合理的；另一方面，则无需指出用于优化神经网络的通用算法可以让人们更快训练出生成 Deepfake 的模型。
- 作者应考虑技术在被按预期使用且正常工作时可能产生的危害、技术被按预期使用但给出错误结果时可能产生的危害，以及（有意或无意）滥用技术所导致的危害。
- 若存在负面社会影响，作者还可讨论可能的缓解策略（例如模型的受控/门控发布、在攻击之外提供防御、监测滥用的机制、监测系统如何随时间从反馈中学习的机制、提升机器学习的效率与可及性）。

> 本块为第 36 页第 10 条目指引的续接部分。

11.Safeguards

**中文:**

### 11. 安全保障（Safeguards）

---

<span id="S037-03"></span>
**Source:** p.37 S037-03

**Original:**

Question: Does the paper describe safeguards that have been put in place for responsible
release of data or models that have a high risk for misuse (e.g., pre-trained language models,
image generators, or scraped datasets)?
Answer: [N/A]
Justification: This paper poses no such risks.

**中文:**

**问题：** 论文是否描述了为负责任地发布可能被滥用的高风险数据或模型（例如预训练语言模型、图像生成器或抓取的数据集）而设置的保障措施？

**回答：** [N/A]

**理由：** 本文不构成此类风险。

---

<span id="S037-04"></span>
**Source:** p.37 S037-04

**Original:**

Guidelines:
• The answer [N/A] means that the paper poses no such risks.
• Released models that have a high risk for misuse or dual-use should be released with
necessary safeguards to allow for controlled use of the model, for example by requiring
that users adhere to usage guidelines or restrictions to access the model or implementing
safety filters.
• Datasets that have been scraped from the Internet could pose safety risks. The authors
should describe how they avoided releasing unsafe images.
• We recognize that providing effective safeguards is challenging, and many papers do
not require this, but we encourage authors to take this into account and make a best
faith effort.

**中文:**

**指引：**

- 回答 [N/A] 表示论文不构成此类风险。
- 具有高滥用风险或双用途风险的已发布模型，应附带必要的保障措施以实现受控使用，例如要求用户遵守使用准则，或对模型访问施加限制，或实现安全过滤器。
- 从互联网抓取的数据集可能带来安全风险。作者应说明如何避免发布不安全的图像。
- 我们认识到提供有效的保障措施具有挑战性，且许多论文并不需要这样做，但我们鼓励作者将其纳入考虑并作出最大诚意的努力。

---

Guidelines:
• The answer [N/A] means that the paper does not use existing assets.
• The authors should cite the original paper that produced the code package or dataset.
• The authors should state which version of the asset is used and, if possible, include a

• The name of the license (e.g., CC-BY 4.0) should be included for each asset.

- 作者应引用产出代码包或数据集的原始论文。
- 作者应说明所使用的资产版本，并在可能时给出 URL。
- 对每项资产都应给出许可证名称（例如 CC-BY 4.0）。
- 对来自特定来源（例如网站）的抓取数据，应提供该来源的版权与使用条款。

> 指引条目续接第 38 页。

[第 39 页](#page-39)

## 术语表

| English | 中文 | 用法说明 |
|---|---|---|
| flow matching | 流匹配 | 以线性插值构造概率路径、用速度场 ODE 采样的生成模型框架 |
| proximal initialization | 近端初始化 | 在源空间把初始噪声刻意放在后验能量极小值附近的初始化方式（第 1 阶段产物） |
| bounded stochasticity | 有界随机性 | 钟形调度 η(t)=λ_s·√(1−σ_t)·σ_t 下受控的累积噪声注入 |
| source space | 源空间 | 由初始噪声 x₁ 张成的空间，x₀=Φ(x₁) |
| trajectory space | 轨迹空间 | 由采样中间状态 x_t 张成的空间 |
| sufficiency regime | 充分性区间 | 近端初始化已达性能平台、无需穷尽源空间优化的邻近度范围（本设置 C₀≤0.4） |
| cumulative stochasticity | 累积随机性 | G:=∫₀¹g_t²dt，直接决定稳定性界中的随机偏离项 d·G |
| posterior energy | 后验能量 | E(x₁):=−log p(x₁\|y)+C，分解为似然项与先验项 |
| one-sided Lipschitz | 单边 Lipschitz 条件 | 只约束雅可比对称部分最大特征值的条件，参数可取负值 |
| Tweedie's formula | Tweedie 公式 | 由含噪中间状态一步估计干净图像：x̂_{0\|t}=x_t−t·v_θ(x_t,t) |
| MCPO | 流形约束近端目标 | Manifold-Constrained Proximal Objective，即带 KL 项与累积量正则的近端目标 J(φ) |
| SWDC | 谱加权数据一致性 | Spectrally Weighted Data Consistency，用 Wiener 滤波按频率加权的一致性损失 |
| SDO | 捷径扩散优化 | Shortcut Diffusion Optimization，仅在首步保留计算图的梯度近似 |
| NFE | 函数评估次数 | number of function evaluations，采样/优化中网络前向调用的总次数 |
| BSS | 有界随机性调度 | bounded stochasticity schedule，即式 (10) 的钟形 η(t) 轮廓 |
| cumulant regularizer | 累积量正则项 | R(μϕ)：通过惩罚偏度、超额峰度等高阶矩偏离来抑制非高斯漂移（§F.4、表 S6） |
| pink noise prior | 粉红噪声先验 | 自然图像的 1/f² 功率谱先验 Sx0(ω)∝∥ω∥^{−α}，用于频谱加权数据一致性（式 8） |
| Wiener filter | 维纳滤波 | 由测量与先验谱构造的线性初值算子，权重记为 W(ω, t_stop) |
| typical set | 典型集 | 高斯先验流形上的高概率区域；噪声离开该集合即 OOD 漂移（§F.6） |
| forward-noising / deterministic inversion / stochastic inversion | 前向加噪 / 确定性反演 / 随机反演 | 三种由伪逆测量导出初始噪声的基线初始化策略（§F.5、表 S7） |
| Tweedie's estimate | Tweedie 估计 | 由速度场给出的 x̂0 闭式估计，用于采样轨迹可视化（图 S4） |
| mode collapse | 模式坍缩 | 后验采样退化为单一解；本文以 Var[x\|y] 验证其不存在（§F.7） |
| inpainting | 图像修复 | 附加任务；箱式修复（box inpainting）见 §F.1 与表 S4 |
| perception–distortion trade-off | 感知—失真权衡 | 保真度指标（PSNR、SSIM）与感知指标（LPIPS、FID）之间的权衡 |
| CFG scale | 无分类器引导尺度 | classifier-free guidance 强度；各基线取值见 §E.2 |
| calibration set | 校准集 | 用于网格搜索基线超参数的数据划分 |
| OOD | 分布外 | out-of-distribution；过度源空间优化使噪声离开先验典型集（§F.6、图 S5） |

## 阅读提示

- 本批（第 1–20 页）分片：p001-003、p004-006、p007-009、p010-012、p013-015、p016-017、p018-020，共 296 块；第 21–39 页由另一位译者完成。
- 阅读主线：第 3 节给出近端初始化与采样随机性的耦合分析（命题 3.1），附录 A 给出完整证明；第 4 节给出两阶段方法（近端源初始化 + 有界随机性调度），第 5 节为实验，附录 C 为实现细节。
- 表 1（p.7）、表 2（p.8）、表 3a/3b（p.9）在 pypdf 抽取中被压平成一整行数字，已用 pdfplumber 按坐标逐格核对并重建为 Markdown 表格，所有数字、单位与行列标题均保留。
- 编号公式均独立成块并用 LaTeX \tag 保留原编号：正文式 (1)–(10)，附录式 (S1)–(S27)；pypdf 对分式、求和号、范数与下括号注解的压平已按语义还原。
- 图 1–图 6 与图 S1 的图注独立成块（id 用 -C 规则）；插图内部的坐标刻度/图例文字是被 pypdf 打散的碎片，另以 type=other 块记录并按图意整理，不计入正文。
- 第 10–13 页的参考文献 [1]–[68] 逐条成块，original_text 保留英文原条目，译文给出中文题名与出处；其中 [26] 与 [27] 在原文中即为同一条目的重复。
- 该 PDF 为单栏 NeurIPS 排版，页面内未出现左右栏交错；跨页断句（p.1→p.2、p.2→p.3、p.3→p.4、p.4→p.5、p.8→p.9、p.18→p.19）已在相邻块的 note 中注明。
- 源文本存在个别拼写/排版瑕疵（posteirior、exessive、Measurment、PNSR、0.4410 等），译文按正确语义理解并在对应块 note 中说明，未改动原文数值。
- 本文件为第 21–39 页（附录 D.2 末尾至 NeurIPS Paper Checklist 结束）的补充术语与阅读提示，供与第 1–20 页的 meta.json 合并去重。
- 第 21 页起进入附录：§E 为 FLINT 与基线的实现细节（表 S1–S3），§F 为附加实验（表 S4–S7、图 S3–S6），§G 与第 28–32 页为定性结果图（图 S7–S11），第 33–39 页为 NeurIPS 论文检查清单。
- 表 S2、S3、S5 在原 PDF 中均为左右两张子表合排（流匹配部分与扩散部分任务列不同），Markdown 中已拆为两张子表并逐行展开合并单元格。
- 图 S3、S7–S10 的列标签为旋转竖排文字，抽取文本被压平且顺序错乱；译文中仅按语义列举方法名，未强行断言列序，详见各块 note。
- 第 26 页表 S7 为右侧 wrap 浮动表，与左栏正文并排；已按「表格—正文」顺序独立成块，正文段落连续完整。
- 第 26–27 页图 S5/S6 的图内坐标轴刻度与色标数值属图形元素，未逐项录入；图 S5(c) 的样本标注数值已在译文中以表格重建。
- 源文本疑似排印不一致：第 20 页正文称 FFHQ 流匹配的初始学习率 αlr = 0.06，而表 S1 对应值为 0.006；译文两处均按原文保留，未擅自统一。
